- परिबाधः (*paribādhaḥ*)—any of the enemies who tried to harm you
- न (*na*)—cannot stop you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, who have horses called Hari, your enemy warriors engaged in battle cannot forbid your left hand at the time when you seek the cows. They cannot stop your right hand either. None of the enemies who try to harm you can stop you.

##### English translation
Lord of steeds, in the recovery of the cattle the opponents resist not your left hand nor your right, your enemies (resist you) not.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॒ गोभि॑रिव व्र॒जं गी॒र्भिर्ऋ॑णोम्यद्रिवः ।  
> आ स्मा॒ कामं॑ जरि॒तुरा मनः॑ पृण ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । गोभिः इव । व्रजं । गीभिः । ऋणोमि । आद्रिवः ।  
> आ । स्म । कामं । जरितुः । आ । मनः । पृण ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अद्रिवो वज्रवन्निंद्र गीर्भिः स्तुतिलक्षणाभिर्वाग्भिस्ता त्वामा ऋणोमि ।
> प्राप्नोमि ॥ ऋणु गतौ । तनादिः ॥ तत्र दृष्टांतः । गोभिरिव । यथा गोपालो
> गोभिर्व्रजं गोष्ठं गच्छति तद्वत्त्वां स्तुतिभिः प्राप्नोमीत्यर्थः । ततस्त्वं जरितुः
> स्तोतुर्मम कामं धनादिविषयमा पृण । आपूरय । तथा मनो मदीयं
> मानसं धनादिप्रदानेनापूरय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O wielder of the thunderbolt (adrivat), O Indra, with praises, with words in the form of praise, I approach you; I reach you. The example: as a cowherd comes with the cows to the cow-pen, to the pasture, so I reach you with praises. This is the meaning. Then fulfil the desire of me, the reciter, concerned with wealth and the like; and likewise fill my mind with the giving of wealth and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्रिवः (*adrivaḥ*)—O Indra, who wield the thunderbolt weapon
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—a cowherd accompanied by cows
- व्रजं इव (*vrajaṃ iva*)—as he joins the cow-pen
- गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)—with words in the form of praise

### Printed p. 79
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- आ ऋणोमि (*ā ṛṇomi*)—I approach with devotion
- जरितुः (*jarituḥ*)—of me the praiser
- कामं (*kāmaṃ*)—the desire concerned with wealth and the like
- आ पृण (*ā pṛṇa*)—fulfil
- मनः आ (*manaḥ ā*)—also my mind, gladden

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, who wield the thunderbolt weapon, as a cowherd with his cows joins the cow-pen, so with words in the form of praise I approach you with devotion. Fulfil the desire of me the praiser, concerned with wealth and the like. Gladden my mind too.

##### English translation
I approach you, thunderer, with praises, as (a cowherd goes) with cattle to the pasture; gratify the desire, satisfy the mind of your adorer.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विश्वा॑नि वि॒श्वम॑नसो धि॒या नो॑ वृत्रहंतम ।  
> उग्र॑ प्रणेत॒रधि॒ षू व॑सो गहि ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विश्वानि । विश्वमनसः । धिया । नः । वृत्रहन्तम ।  
> उग्र । प्रनेतरिति प्रनेशः । अधि । सु । वसो इति । गहि ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृत्रहंतमातिशयेन वृत्रासुरस्योपद्रवाणां वा हंतः । नाद्भस्यॆति
> तमपो नुडागमः ॥ कीदृश । उग्रोद्गर्णबल प्रणेतः स्तोत्कृणां प्रकर्षण
> धनादेर्नेतः ॥ आमंत्रित इत्युग्रेत्यस्याविद्यमानवद्भावप्रतिषेधः ॥ तथाविध वसो
> शक्रूणां वासयितरिंद्र नः । पूजायां बहुवचनं । विश्वमनस एतन्नाम्यो मम
> विश्वानि सर्वाणि स्तोत्राणि कर्माणि वा धिया मनसा सु सुष्पधि गहि । अधिगच्छ ।
> स्तुत्यतया यष्टव्यतया वा मनोवेगेन गच्छेत्यर्थः ॥ गमेर्लोटि छांदसः
> शपो लुक् । हेर्जिद्वद्भावादनुदात्तोपदेशेत्यनुनासिकलोपः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O foremost slayer of Vṛtra, that is, the greatest slayer of the asura Vṛtra or of troubles. Of what kind? O fierce one (ugra), possessed of formidable strength, O praṇetaḥ, one who leads forward wealth and the like to the reciters. Such as you are, O vasu, O Indra, who make enemies dwell (vāsayitṛ): the plural "us" (naḥ) is used in honour. All praises or rites of me, named Viśvamanas, come near with the mind, well; come to them. The meaning is: as one to be praised or as one to be worshipped, come with the speed of the mind.

### Printed p. 80

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृत्रहंतम (*vṛtrahaṃtama*)—O one who slays Vṛtra to the highest degree
- उग्र (*ugra*)—one of exceedingly great strength
- प्रणीतः (*praṇītaḥ*)—the best leader of the reciters
- वसो (*vaso*)—one who overthrows enemies, O Indra
- विश्वमनसः (*viśvamanasaḥ*)—named Viśvamanas
- नः (*naḥ*)—my
- विश्वानि (*viśvāni*)—all rites, turned towards them
- धिया (*dhiyā*)—with the speed of the mind
- सु (*su*)—swiftly
- आधि गहि (*ādhi gahi*)—be gracious

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, who slay enemies to the highest degree, who are of exceedingly great strength, the best leader of the reciters, who overthrow enemies: be gracious swiftly, with the speed of the mind, turning towards all the rites of me, who am named Viśvamanas.

##### English translation
Fierce destroyer of Vritra, bringer (of wealth to your worshippers), subduer of enemies, preside over all (the offerings) of us, Vishwamanas, with a (favourable) mind.
#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> व॒यं ते॑ अ॒स्य वृ॑त्रहन्वि॒द्याम॑ शूर॒ नव्य॑सः ।  
> वसोः॑ स्मा॒र्हस्य॑ पुरुहूत॒ राध॑सः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वयं । ते । अस्य । वृत्रहन् । विद्याम । शूर । नव्यसः ।  
> वसोः । स्पार्हस्य । पुरुहूत । राधसः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृत्रहन् वृत्रस्य हंतः शूर बलवन् पुरुहूत पुरुभिर्बहुभिराह्वातव्येंद्र
> नव्यसो नवीयसः ॥ ईयसुन ईकारलोपश्यांदसः ॥ नवतरं स्वाह्यस्य स्पृहणीयं
> राधसः ॥ राध साध संसिद्धौ ॥ शर्मादेः संसाधकं ते त्वदीयमस्य वसोः ॥
> क्रियाग्रहणं कर्तव्यमिति वसोः संप्रदानसंज्ञा । चतुर्थ्यर्थे बहुलं छंदसीति
> वसोः षष्ठी ॥ त्वदीयमिदं परिदृश मानं धनं वयं विद्याम । लभेमहि ॥ विद्लृ
> लाभे ! अदादिकः । छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O slayer of Vṛtra (Vṛtrahan), that is, slayer of Vṛtra; O hero (śūra), O mighty one; O Indra, called by many (purūhūta). नव्यसो (*navyaso*) means "newer" (navīyasaḥ). राधसः (*rādhasaḥ*) is derived from the root rādh/sādh in the sense of accomplishing, so it means that which accomplishes happiness and the like; it is desirable. ते (*te*) means "yours", and वसोः (*vasoḥ*) ("wealth") is here in the genitive in the sense of the dative. We obtain (विद्याम (*vidyāma*), that is, may we gain) this wealth of yours that is seen here.
### Printed p. 81

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृत्रहन् (*vṛtrahan*)—destroyer of enemies
- शूर (*śūra*)—endowed with power
- पुरुहूत (*puruhūta*)—worthy of being invoked by many, O Indra
- नव्यसः (*navyasaḥ*)—that which is new
- स्पार्हस्य (*spārhasya*)—that which is attractive
- राधसः (*rādhasaḥ*)—that which accomplishes what is auspicious
- ते (*te*)—your
- अस्य वसोः (*asya vasoḥ*)—this wealth
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- विद्याम (*vidyāma*)—may we obtain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, destroyer of enemies, endowed with power, worthy of being invoked by many: may we obtain this wealth of yours, which is new, attractive and a bringer of happiness.

##### English translation
May we, hero, slayer of Vritra, invoked of many, become possessed of this your new, desirable, and happiness-producing wealth.
#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॒ यथा॒ ह्यस्ति॒ तेऽप॑रीतं नृतो॒ शवः॑ ।  
> अमृ॑क्ता रा॒तिः पु॑रुहूत दा॒शुषे॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । यथा । हि । अस्ति । ते । अपरिऽइतं । नृतो इति । शवः ।  
> अमृक्ता । रातिः । पुरुहूत । दाशुषे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे नृतो सर्वस्यांतर्यामितया नर्तयितरिंद्र ते त्वदीयं शवो बलं
> यथापरीतमस्ति शत्रुभिरपरिगतमव्याप्तं भवति । हि प्रसिद्धौ । तथा हे पुरुहूत
> पुरुभिर्बहुभिराहूतेंद्र दाशुषे हविर्गत्तवशे यजमानाय रातिर्धनादिदानममृक्ता शत्रुभिरहिंसितं भवति । त्वत्तो लब्धं यजमानस्य धनं शत्रुवो स हिंसंति ।
> यथा त्वदीयबलस्य रक्षक एवं तस्य धनस्यापि रक्षक इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O नृतो (*nṛto*), O Indra, who make all dance as the inner controller of all: your strength is unassailed (अपरीतं (*aparītaṃ*)), that is, not encompassed or overcome by enemies; हि (*hi*) is used in the sense of "well known". In the same way, O Purūhūta, called by many, the gift (रातिः (*rātiḥ*)) of wealth and the like to the sacrificer, who has made the offering of oblation, is unharmed (अमृक्ता (*amṛktā*)) by enemies. The wealth received from you by the sacrificer is not injured by enemies. The meaning is: as you are the protector of your own strength, so you are also the protector of his wealth. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नृतो (*nṛto*)—one who is the inner controller in all and impels all
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- शवः (*śavaḥ*)—strength
- यथा (*yathā*)—in whatever manner
- अपरीतं (*aparītaṃ*)—unopposed by enemies
### Printed p. 82
- अस्ति हि (*asti hi*)—it is (in that same manner)
- पुरुहूत (*puruhūta*)—O Indra, worthy of being invoked by many
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the sacrificer who offers oblation
- रातिः (*rātiḥ*)—the wealth that you give
- अमृक्ता (*amṛktā*)—does not fall prey to the harm of enemies

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, the inner controller in all, who impel all: in whatever manner your strength is unopposed by enemies, in that same manner, O Indra, worthy of being invoked by many, the wealth that you give to the sacrificer who offers oblation does not fall prey to the harm of enemies.

##### English translation
As Indra, inspirer (of men), your strength is irresistible, (so) invoked of many, your munificence to the donor (of oblations) cannot be marred.
#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ वृ॑षस्व महामह म॒हे नृ॑तम॒ राध॑से ।  
> दृ॒ळश्चि॑द्दृह्य मघवन्म॒घत्त॑ये ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । वृषस्व । महामह । महे । नृतम । राधसे ।  
> दृढः । चित् । दृढ । मघवन् । मघत्तये ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे महामहातिशयेन सर्वैः पूजनीय नृतम नेतृतमेंद्र महे
> महते राधसे शत्रुधनानां संसाधकाय बलाय बलार्थमा वृषस्व । स्वोदरमासिंच । सोमं पिबेत्यर्थः । हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र सोमपानेन मत्तः सन्
> दृळश्चिद्दृढानि परैरबाधितान्यपि शत्रुपुराणि मघत्तये मघानां धनानां लाभाय
> दृह्य । जिघांस । विदारयेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Mahāmaha, worshipful by all to the highest degree, O नृतम (*nṛtama*), the best leader, Indra: pour (आ वृषस्व (*ā vṛṣasva*)) into your own belly, for great (महे (*mahe*)) राधसे (*rādhase*), that is, for strength which accomplishes the seizing of the enemies' wealth; the meaning is, drink the Soma. O मघवन् (*maghavan*), wealthy Indra, intoxicated by drinking Soma, destroy (दृह्य (*dṛhya*), that is, wish to slay, tear apart) the strong (दृढानि (*dṛḍhāni*)) cities of the enemies, even those not broken by others, for the gain of riches (मघत्तये (*maghattaye*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महामह (*mahāmaha*)—worthy of exceedingly great worship
- नृतम (*nṛtama*)—the excellent leader, O Indra
- महे (*mahe*)—great
- राधसे (*rādhase*)—to carry off the enemy's wealth (to drink the Soma that gives strength)
### Printed p. 83
- आ वृषस्व (*ā vṛṣasva*)—open your belly and drink the Soma
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, possessor of wealth
- दृळश्चित् (*dṛḷaścit*)—even the very strong cities of the enemy
- मघवत्तये (*maghavattaye*)—for the carrying off of the enemy's wealth
- दृह्य (*dṛhya*)—destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, worthy of exceedingly great worship, the excellent leader: open your belly to drink the Soma that gives strength to carry off great wealth of the enemy. Drink the Soma. O Indra, possessor of wealth, destroy even the very strong cities of the enemy, to carry off the enemy's wealth.

##### English translation
Most adorable, chief leader (of men), invigorate (yourself with the Soma) for (the conquest of) great wealth : consume, Magavan, the strong (cities of the Asuras) for the rich spoil.
#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू अ॒न्यत्रा॑ चिदद्रिव॒स्वन्नो॑ जग्मुरा॒शसः॑ ।  
> मघ॑वङ्॒ग्धि तव॒ तन्न॑ ऊ॒तिभिः॑ ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । अन्यत्र । चित् । अद्रिवः । त्वत् । नः । जग्मुः । आशसः ।  
> मघवन् । शग्नि । तव । तत् । नः । ऊतिभिः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अद्रिवो वज्रवन्निंद्र त्वं धनवान् दाता चेत्यपरिज्ञाय नोऽस्मदीयान्याशस आशंसनान्यभिलाषास्त्वत्त्वत्तोऽन्यत्र देवादौ नू चित् पुरा जग्मुः । अगच्छन् ।
> तत्र फलं नालभंत । इदानीं त्वं धनवान् दानशील इत्यस्माभिर्ज्ञातं । अत एव हे
> मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र तव त्वदीयं तच्छत्रुपुरविदारणलब्धं धनमूतिभिस्त्वद्रक्षणैर्नोऽस्मभ्यं शग्धि । देहि ॥ शग्धीति दानकर्मा । शक्नोतेर्लोटि छांदसो विकरणस्य
> लुक् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O अद्रिवो (*adrivo*), Indra, wielder of the thunderbolt: not knowing that you are wealthy and a giver, our desires (आशस (*āśasa*), that is, wishes and longings) went formerly (नू चित् (*nū cit*)) elsewhere than to you, to other gods and the like, and obtained no fruit there. Now we have learned that you are wealthy and generous. For that very reason, O मघवन् (*maghavan*), wealthy Indra, give (शग्धि (*śagdhi*)) us that wealth of yours, won by the tearing open of the enemies' cities, with your protections. शग्धीति (*śagdhīti*) is a word meaning giving.
### Printed p. 84

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्रिवः (*adrivaḥ*)—O Indra, wielder of the thunderbolt (before we came to know that you are the giver of wealth)
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- आशसः (*āśasaḥ*)—desires
- त्वत् (*tvat*)—than you
- अन्यत्र (*anyatra*)—in other gods
- नू चित् (*nū cit*)—formerly
- जग्मुः (*jagmuḥ*)—went and joined (it has only now been learned that you alone are the giver of wealth; therefore)
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, possessor of wealth
- तव (*tava*)—your
- तत् (*tat*)—that wealth obtained from the enemies
- ऊतिभिः (*ūtibhiḥ*)—with your protections
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- शग्धि (*śagdhi*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, wielder of the thunderbolt: before we knew you to be the giver of wealth, our desires went and joined other gods than you. It has only now been learned that you alone give wealth. Therefore, O Indra, possessor of wealth, give us, with your protections, the wealth obtained from the enemies.

##### English translation
Wielder of the thunderbolt, our solicitations have been formerly addressed to other gods than you : give us, Maghavan, of your (spoil, and guard it) for us with (your) protections.
#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒ह्य१॒ं॑ १ ग नृ॑तो॒ त्वद॒न्यं विं॒दामि॒ राध॑से ।  
> रा॒ये द्यु॒म्नाय॒ शव॑से च गिर्वणः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । अंग ' नृतो इति । त्वत् । अन्यं । विंदामि । राधसे ।  
> राये । द्युम्नाय । शवसे । च । गीर्वणः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे नृतो नर्तयितर्गीर्वणो गीर्भिः स्तुतिभिर्वननीय संभजनीयेंद्र
> राधसे बलसंसाधकायान्नाय राये धनाय द्युम्नाय द्योतमानाय यशसे
> शवसे वर्धकाय बलाय च त्वत्त्वत्तोऽन्यं नहि विंदामि । न लभे । अंग प्रसिद्धौ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O नृतो (*nṛto*), who make all dance, O गीर्वणो (*gīrvaṇo*), Indra, who are to be loved and served with praises (गीर्भिः (*gīrbhiḥ*)): for राधसे (*rādhase*), that is, food which accomplishes strength; for राये (*rāye*), wealth; for द्युम्नाय (*dyumnāya*), shining fame; and for शवसे (*śavase*), strength which increases: I find (विंदामि (*viṃdāmi*)), that is, I obtain, no other than you. अंग (*aṃga*) is used in the sense of "well known".
### Printed p. 85

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नृतो (*nṛto*)—impeller of all
- गिर्वण (*girvaṇa*)—worshipped with praises, O Indra
- राधसे (*rādhase*)—for food that accomplishes strength
- राये (*rāye*)—for wealth
- द्युम्नाय (*dyumnāya*)—for shining fame
- शवसे च (*śavase ca*)—and for the increase of strength
- त्वत् (*tvat*)—apart from you
- अन्यं (*anyaṃ*)—another deity
- नहि विंदामि (*nahi viṃdāmi*)—I do not approach
- अंग (*aṃga*)—this is certain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O impeller of all, worshipped with praises, O Indra: for food that accomplishes strength, for wealth, for shining fame and also for the increase of strength, I approach no deity other than you. This is certain.

##### English translation
Verily, impeller (of men), you are to be worshipped by praise; I apply to no other than you for sustenance, riches, reputation, and strength.
#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एंदु॒मिंद्रा॑य सिंचत॒ सिबा॑ति सो॒म्यं मधु॑ ।  
> प्र राध॑सा चोदयाते महित्व॒ना ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इंदुं । इंद्राय । सिंचत । पिबाति । सोम्यं । मधु ।  
> प्र । राधसा । चोदयाते । महित्वना ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋत्विजः इंदुं स्यंदनशीलं सोममिंद्रार्थमा सिंचत । आश्रयणद्रव्येण सेचनं कुरुत । अभिषुतेत्यर्थः । ततः सोम्यं सोममयं मधु
> मधुकरं सोमरसं पिबाति । पिबतु । पीत्वा च स इंद्रो महित्वना स्वमहत्वेनैव
> राधसान्नेन सह धनादिकं स्तोतृभ्यः प्र चोदयाते । प्रकर्षेण चोदयति ।
> यद्वा । यजमानो महित्वना । इंद्राय प्रदीयमानत्पादस्य महत्त्वं । महत्त्वयुक्तन राधसान्नेन सह स्तोतृन् प्रचोदयति । इंद्राय हविर्दत्तेति
> स्तोतृन्प्रेरयतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O priests, pour (सिंचत (*siṃcata*)) the flowing (इंदुं (*iṃduṃ*)) Soma for Indra, that is, perform the sprinkling with the vessel material; the meaning is, press the Soma. Then let him drink (पिबाति (*pibāti*), that is, let him drink) the Soma-made honey, the Soma juice. Having drunk, Indra, by his own greatness (महित्वना (*mahitvanā*)), together with राधसा (*rādhasā*), that is, food, impels (चोदयाते (*codayāte*), that is, urges forcefully) wealth and the like towards the reciters. Or else: the sacrificer, by greatness (महित्वना (*mahitvanā*)), that is, the greatness of what is being given to Indra, together with food endowed with greatness, urges the reciters on; the meaning is that he impels the reciters, saying "an oblation has been given to Indra". **NOTE**

### Printed p. 86

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—(O priests) with Indra as the object
- इंदुं (*iṃduṃ*)—the flowing Soma
- आ सिंचतॆ (*ā siṃcatè*)—have it sprinkled together with the receiving substances
- सोम्यं (*somyaṃ*)—that which is made of Soma
- मधु (*madhu*)—and honey-making, the Soma juice
- पिबाति (*pibāti*)—let Indra drink
- महित्वना (*mahitvanā*)—joined with his own majesty
- राधसा (*rādhasā*)—with food
- प्र चोदयाते (*pra codayāte*)—he gives encouragement to the reciters

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, have the flowing Soma sprinkled with the receiving substances, with Indra as the object. Let Indra drink the Soma juice, which is made of Soma and honey-making. With food joined with his own majesty, Indra gives encouragement to the reciters.

##### English translation
Effuse the Soma juice for Indra, let him quaff the Soma beverage he by his might rewards (the donor) with wealth.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The commentator states that the meaning यजमानो महित्वना (*yajamāno mahitvanā*) is also possible. The purport is that the sacrificer, with food joined with his own greatness, urges the reciters on for the worship of Indra.
#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उपो॒ हरी॑णां॒ पतिं॒ दक्षं॑ पृं॒चंत॑मब्रुवं ।  
> नू॒नं श्रु॑धि स्तुव॒तो अ॒श्वस्य॑ ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उपो इति । हरीणां । पतिं । दक्षं । पृंचंतं । अब्रवं ।  
> नूनं । श्रुधि । स्तुवतः । अश्वस्य ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हरीणां हरितवर्णानामश्वानां पतिं पालयितारं दक्षं वर्धकं स्वबलं
> पृंचंतं ॥ पृची संपर्के ॥ मरुत्सु योजयंतं । यद्वा । शत्रुषु स्वबलमा-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra is the lord and protector of the tawny-coloured horses (हरीणां (*harīṇāṃ*)), the increaser (दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*)) of strength, who joins (पृंचंतं (*pṛṃcaṃtaṃ*), from the root meaning "to connect") his own strength with the Maruts. Or else, he is one who ranges, with his own strength, against the enemies
### Printed p. 87

> युधादिभिः संचर्यंतं । एतादृशमिंद्रं त्वामुपो अब्रवं । विश्वमना अहं
> स्तोत्रं करवाणि । अश्वस्य । व्यश्वो नामर्षिरश्वशब्देनोच्यते । तस्य पुस्रस्य स्तुवतः
> स्तोत्रं कुर्वतो मम संबंधिनीं त्वद्विषयां स्तुतिं नूनं संप्रति श्रुधि । शृणु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

with weapons and the like. To you, such an Indra, I, Viśvamanas, address (उपो अब्रवं (*upo abravaṃ*)): let me make praise. अश्वस्य (*aśvasya*): the seer named Vyaśva is spoken of by the word aśva; of his son, who am praising and making a hymn, hear now (नूनं (*nūnaṃ*), that is, at present) my praise concerning you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हरीणां (*harīṇāṃ*)—to the horses of tawny colour
- पतिं (*patiṃ*)—the protector
- दक्षं (*dakṣaṃ*)—the increaser of strength
- पृंचंतं (*pṛṃcaṃtaṃ*)—Indra, who joins his strength with the Maruts
- उपो अब्रवं (*upo abravaṃ*)—I praise and extol (O Indra)
- स्तुवतः (*stuvataḥ*)—the praiser
- अश्वस्य (*aśvasya*)—my praise, who am the son of Vyaśva and named Viśvamanas
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I praise and extol Indra, the protector of the tawny-coloured horses, the increaser of strength, who joins his strength with the Maruts. O Indra, hear at this time the praise of me, the praiser, the son of Vyaśva, named Viśvamanas.

##### English translation
Let me address the lord of steeds, who associates his strength (with the Maruts) : now hear the words of the son of Vyashwa praising you.
#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒ह्य१॒ं॑ १ ग पु॒रा च॒न ज॒ज्ञे वी॒रत॑र॒स्त्वत् ।  
> नकी॑ रा॒या नैवथा॒ न भं॒दना॑ ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । अंग । पुरा । चन । जज्ञे । वीरतरः । त्वत् ।  
> नकिः । राया । न । एवथा । न । भंदना ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वत्त्वत्तः पुरा पूर्वं वीरतरः सामर्थ्यवान् कश्चिन्नहि जज्ञे ।
> जातः खलु । अंग प्रसिद्धौ । त्वमेव सामर्थ्यवाञ्चित इत्यर्थः । किंच त्वत्तोऽपि
> राया धनेन समर्थो नकिर्न कश्चिदस्ति । तथैवथा शत्रुपुराणि संग्रामं वा प्रति

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, no one more heroic, that is, more capable, than you was born before (पुरा (*purā*)); अंग (*aṃga*) is used in the sense of "well known". The meaning is that you alone are capable. Moreover, no one is as competent as you in wealth (राया (*rāyā*)). Likewise, with regard to एवथा (*evathā*), towards the enemies' cities or towards battle
### Printed p. 88

> गमनेन त्वत्तोऽधिको न जातः । यद्वा । एवथा ॥ अव रक्षणादिषु । अकारस्यॆकारश्नांदसः । औणादिकोऽथप्रत्ययः ॥ शरणागतानां स्तोत्कृणां चावनेन त्वत्तोऽधिको नास्ति । किंच बंधना । भंदतिः स्तुतिकर्मा । स्तुत्या च त्वदधिको न जातः ।
> धनवान्रक्षकः स्तुत्यश्च त्वत्तोऽन्नो न जज्ञ इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

in going, no one superior to you has been born. Or else, एवथा (*evathā*) is from the root अव (*ava*) in the senses of protecting and the like; the change of a to e is Vedic; the suffix is one of the Uṇādi kind. In protecting those who have come for refuge and the reciters, there is none greater than you. Moreover, बंधना (*baṃdhanā*): the root भंदतिः (*bhaṃdatiḥ*) has the sense of praise; in praise also no one greater than you has been born. The meaning is that no other, wealthy, protecting and praiseworthy, has been born than you. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- त्वत् (*tvat*)—(O Indra) than you
- पुरा चन (*purā cana*)—before
- वीरतरः (*vīrataraḥ*)—one of greater capacity
- न हि जज्ञे (*na hi jajñe*)—was not born at all
- अंग (*aṃga*)—this is certain
- राया (*rāyā*)—one more excellent than you in wealth
- नकिः (*nakiḥ*)—there is surely no one
- एवथा (*evathā*)—one who rushes upon the enemies
- न (*na*)—there is no one equal to you
- भंदना (*bhaṃdanā*)—one worthy of praise
- न (*na*)—there is no one equal to you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, no one of greater capacity than you was born before. This is certain. Surely there is no one more excellent than you in wealth. There is no one as valiant as you in rushing upon the enemies. There is no one like you even in being worthy of praise.

##### English translation
No one, Indra, has ever been born more mighty than you; no one (surpassing you) in riches; no one (more powerful) in protection; no one (more entitled) to praise.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The meaning "intent on the protection of those who have come for refuge" is also possible for एवथा (*evathā*). The commentary explains that to the root अव (*ava*), which has the sense of protecting, the Vedic letter e is added, and that एवथा (*evathā*) has come in place of अवथा (*avathā*).
#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एदु॒ मध्यो॑ म॒दिंत॑रं सिं॒च वा॑ध्वयो॒ अंध॑सः ।  
> ए॒वा हि वी॒रः स्तव॑ते स॒दावृ॑धः ॥ १६ ॥

### Printed p. 89

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इत् । ऊं इति । मध्वः । मदिन्तरं । सिंच । वा । अध्वयो इति ।  
> अंधसः ।  
> एव । हि । वीरः । स्तवते । सदावृधः ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऐदु मध्व इति तृचः पूर्वोक्ते ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिशस्त्रे वैकल्पिकोऽनुरूपः ।
> सूत्रितं च । ऐदु मध्वो मदिंतरमेतो न्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः । आ. ७-८ । इति ॥
> हे अध्वर्यो अध्वरस्य नेतर्यत्विक् सदावृधः सर्वदा वृद्धिमतो मध्वो
> मदकरस्यांधसः सोमलक्षणस्यान्नस्य मदिंतरमत्यर्थं मादयितृतमं सोमरसमेवा सिंचेंद्रार्थं । आयमेवेंद्रः स्तवते हि । स्तोत्रशस्त्रादिभिः स्तूयते खलु ।
> स्तुतायेंद्राय सोमो दाशव्यः । तस्मादा सिंचेति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The tṛca beginning ऐदु मध्व (*aidu madhva*) is the alternative anurūpa in the śastra of the Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin spoken of before. It is also laid down in the sūtra: ऐदु मध्वो मदिंतरमेतो न्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः । (*aidu madhvo madiṃtarameto nviṃdraṃ stavāma sakhāyaḥ |*) (Ā. 7-8). O Adhvaryu, priest, leader of the sacrifice: pour (सिंच (*siṃca*)) for Indra the Soma juice itself, the most exhilarating (मदिंतरं (*madiṃtaraṃ*), that is, the greatest gladdener) of the intoxicating (मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)) food (अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)) that has the nature of Soma, for him who is ever growing (सदावृधः (*sadāvṛdhaḥ*)). This very Indra is praised (स्तवते (*stavate*)) with stotras, śastras and the like. The Soma is to be given to Indra who is praised; therefore the connection is "pour".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अध्वयो (*adhvayo*)—O Adhvaryu
- वीरः (*vīraḥ*)—the capable
- सदावृधः (*sadāvṛdhaḥ*)—he who always grows by oblations
- एव हि (*eva hi*)—this very Indra
- स्तवते (*stavate*)—is praised (therefore)
- मध्वः (*madhvaḥ*)—intoxicating
- अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)—of the Soma form
- मदिंतरं (*madiṃtaraṃ*)—the joy-producing juice
- आ सिंचॆ (*ā siṃcè*)—press for the sake of Indra

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Adhvaryu: this very Indra, the capable, who always grows by oblations, is praised by all. Therefore press, for the sake of Indra, the joy-producing juice of the intoxicating Soma.

##### English translation
Pour out, priest, the most exhilarating (draught) of the sweet (sacrificial) beverage, for he, the ever-mighty hero, alone is praised.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

This Ṛk इदु मध्वः (*idu madhvaḥ*) is to be recited as the alternative anurūpa tṛca in the śastra mantras recited by the priest called Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin, mentioned before; this is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana-śrauta-sūtra, ऐदु मध्यो मदिंतरमेतोन्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः (*aidu madhyo madiṃtarametonviṃdraṃ stavāma sakhāyaḥ*) (Ā. 7-8).
### Printed p. 90
#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॑ स्थातर्हरीणां॒ नकि॑ष्टे पू॒र्वस्तु॑तिं ।  
> उदा॑नंश॒ शव॑सा॒ न भं॒दना॑ ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्र । सातः । हरीणां । नकिः । ते । पूर्व्यस्तुतिं ।  
> उत् । आनंश । शवसा । न । छंदना ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे हरीणां स्थातरश्वानामधिष्ठातरिंद्र ते त्वदीयां पूर्वस्तुतिं पूर्वर्यषिभिः कृतां स्तुतिं । उपलक्षणं । इदानींतनैः क्रियमाणामपि स्तुतिं नकिर्न
> कश्चिच्छवसा बलेनोदानंश । व्याप्प्नोति ॥ अशू व्याप्तौ । लिट्यश्नोतेश्वेति नुट् ।
> छांदसो मुम् ॥ कश्चिन्नातिक्रामतीत्यर्थः । किंच भंदना सर्वैः प्रार्थनीयत्वात्
> पूजनीयेन धनेन स्तुत्या वा त्वदीयां स्तुतिं न कश्चिदतिक्रामति । त्वत्तो बलवान्
> धनी स्तुत्यो वान्यो नास्तीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, lord (स्थातर् (*sthātar*)) of the horses, the ancient praise of yours made by the earlier seers, which is a mere indication: no one (नकिः (*nakiḥ*)) surpasses by strength (शवसा (*śavasā*)) even the praise being made by present-day people; it pervades (उदानंश (*udānaṃśa*)); the meaning is that no one goes beyond it. Moreover, with भंदना (*bhaṃdanā*), that is, with wealth worthy of worship because it is sought by all, or with praise, no one goes beyond your praise. The meaning is that there is no other than you who is more powerful, wealthy or praiseworthy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हरीणां (*harīṇāṃ*)—to the horses named Hari
- स्थातः (*sthātaḥ*)—lord
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- पूर्वस्तुतिं (*pūrvastutiṃ*)—the praise made by the former seers (and likewise the modern praise)
- शवसा (*śavasā*)—by strength
- नकिः उदानंश (*nakiḥ udānaṃśa*)—no one can surpass
- भंदना (*bhaṃdanā*)—either by worshipful wealth or by praise
- न (*na*)—no one can surpass

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, lord of the horses named Hari: no one can surpass your praise made by the former seers, and likewise the modern praise, either by strength, by wealth or by praise.

##### English translation
Indra, ruler of horses, no one surpasses your ancient praise, either for force or for fame.
### Printed p. 91
#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं प्रो॒ वाजा॑नां॒ पति॒महू॑महि श्रव॒स्यवः॑ ।  
> अप्रा॑युभिर्य॒ज्ञेभि॑र्वावृ॒धेन्यं॑ ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । वः । वाजानां । पतिं । अहूमहि । श्रवस्यवः ।  
> अप्रायुभिः । यज्ञेभिः । ववृधेन्यं ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अप्रायुभिः कमस्वप्रमाद्यन्मनुष्ययुक्तैः । अथवा । अप्रमत्ता एकत्र स्थितैव
> कर्म कुर्वंति । कर्म प्रारभ्य नान्यं देशं गच्छंतीत्यर्थः । एवंविधमनुष्ययुक्तैर्यज्ञेभिर्यज्ञैः । एतादृशमनुष्यैर्यज्ञैर्वा नावृधेन्यं वर्धनीयं
> वाजानामन्नानां पतिं स्वामिनं वो यष्टयष्टव्यसंबंधेन युष्मदीयं तं
> तादृशमिंद्रं श्रवस्यवो वयमन्नकामाः संतोऽहूमहि । आह्वयामः ॥ ह्वयतेर्लुजि बहुलं छंदसीति संप्रसारणं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

अप्रायुभिः (*aprāyubhiḥ*): by men endowed with unfailing attention. **NOTE** Or else, those who are not careless stay in one place and perform the rite; having begun the rite, they go to no other place. By the sacrifices (यज्ञेभिः (*yajñebhiḥ*)) of men so endowed, or of such men, Indra is to be magnified (वावृधेन्यं (*vāvṛdhenyaṃ*)), the lord (पतिं (*patiṃ*)) of foods (वाजानां (*vājānāṃ*)), yours (वः (*vaḥ*)) through the relation of sacrificer and what is to be sacrificed. We, desirous of food (श्रवस्यवो (*śravasyavo*)), invoke (अहूमहि (*ahūmahi*), that is, call) that Indra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अप्रायुभिः (*aprāyubhiḥ*)—having sacrificers endowed with a single-minded attention, free from heedlessness
- यज्ञेभिः (*yajñebhiḥ*)—by sacrifices
- वावृधेन्यं (*vāvṛdhenyaṃ*)—one who grows
- वाजानां (*vājānāṃ*)—of foods
- पतिं (*patiṃ*)—the lord
- वः (*vaḥ*)—and belonging to all of you
- तं (*taṃ*)—that same Indra
- अवस्यवः (*avasyavaḥ*)—as seekers of protection **NOTE**
- अहूमहि (*ahūmahi*)—we call with prayer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Through sacrifices whose sacrificers are free from heedlessness and endowed with single-minded attention, that same Indra, who grows, the lord of foods, who belongs to all of you, is called by us with prayer, as seekers of protection.

##### English translation
Desirous of food, we invoke your lord of viands, who is to be magnified by sacrifices (offered) by attentive (worshippers).

### Printed p. 92

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

अप्रायुभिः (*aprāyubhiḥ*): the purport is, having sacrificers who, having begun the rite, do not turn to other places but perform the rite with their gaze fixed on that one rite.
#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एतो॒ न्विंद्रं॒ स्तवा॑म॒ सखा॑यः॒ स्तोम्यं॒ नरं॑ ।  
> कृ॒ष्टर्यो विश्वा॑ अ॒भ्यस्येक॒ इत् ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एतो इति । नु । इंद्रं । स्तवाम । सखायः । स्तोम्यं । नरं ।  
> कृष्टीः । यः । विश्वाः । अभि । अस्ति । एकः । इत् ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वोक्त एव शस्त्र एतो न्विंद्रमित्येत् तृचौ वैकल्पिकौ स्तोत्रियानुरूपौ ।
> सूत्रितं च । एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः स्तुहींद्रं व्यश्ववत् । आ. ७-८ । इति ॥
> हे सखायः समानख्याना मित्रभूता ना हे ऋत्विजः नु क्षिप्रमेतो ।
> आगच्छतैव । किमर्थं तदाह । स्तोम्यं स्तोमार्हं नरं सर्वस्य नेतारं तमिंद्रं
> स्तवाम । स्तोत्रं करवाम । य इंद्र एक इदेकोऽसहाय एव सन् विश्वाः सर्वाः
> कृष्टीः शत्रुसेना अभ्यस्ति अभिभवति । तं स्तवामेति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the same śastra spoken of before, the two tṛcas beginning एतो न्विंद्रं (*eto nviṃdraṃ*) are the alternative stotriya and anurūpa. It is also laid down in the sūtra: एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः स्तुहींद्रं व्यश्ववत् (*eto nviṃdraṃ stavāma sakhāyaḥ stuhīṃdraṃ vyaśvavat*) (Ā. 7-8). O friends (सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)), that is, friends of the same fame, O priests, come (एतो (*eto*)) quickly (नु (*nu*)). For what purpose? To praise Indra, who is worthy of the stoma (स्तोम्यं (*stomyaṃ*)), the leader (नरं (*naraṃ*)) of all; let us make praise (स्तवाम (*stavāma*)). That Indra who, one and alone, without a helper, overcomes all (विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)) the hostile armies (कृष्टीः (*kṛṣṭīḥ*)): the remainder is "let us praise him".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—O priests, who are friends
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- एक इत् (*eka it*)—as the unequalled one
- विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)—all
- कृष्टीः (*kṛṣṭīḥ*)—the hostile armies
- अभ्यस्ति (*abhyasti*)—overthrows and defeats, such
- स्तोम्यं (*stomyaṃ*)—one worthy of worship
- नरं (*naraṃ*)—and a leader
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- स्तवाम (*stavāma*)—let us praise
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- एतो (*eto*)—be pleased to come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, who are friends: let us praise that Indra, worthy of worship and a leader, who, unequalled, overthrows and defeats all the hostile armies. Be pleased to come quickly.
### Printed p. 93

##### English translation
Come, friends, let us glorify Indra, the leader, who is entitled to praise, who, single, overcomes all hostile hosts.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

These two tṛcas (six Ṛks) beginning एतो न्विंद्रं (*eto nviṃdraṃ*) are to be recited in the śastra mantras spoken of before as the alternative stotriya and the anurūpa tṛcas. This is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana-śrauta-sūtra, एतो न्विंद्रं स्तवाम सखायः स्तुहींद्रं व्यश्ववत् (*eto nviṃdraṃ stavāma sakhāyaḥ stuhīṃdraṃ vyaśvavat*) (Ā. 7-8).
#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अगो॑रुधाय ग॒विषे॑ द्यु॒क्षाय॒ दस्म्यं॒ वचः॑ ।  
> घृ॒तात्स्वादी॑यो॒ मधु॑नश्च वोचत ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अगोरुधाय । गोइषे । द्युक्षाय । दस्म्यं । वचः ।  
> स्फुतात् । स्वादीयः । मधुनः । च । वोचक ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋत्विजः अगोरुधाय । गाः स्तुती रुणद्दीति गोरुधः । न गोरुधोऽगोरुधः । ता न विनाशयत्यादरेण शृणोतीत्यर्थः । तादृशाय अत एव गविषे
> स्तोत्राणीच्यते द्युक्षाय दीप्यमानायेंद्राय दस्म्यं दर्शनीयं धृतात्सादुतरादाज्यान्मधुनश्च स्वादीयोऽतिशयेन स्वादुभूतं वचः स्तोत्ररूपं वाक्यं
> वोचत । ब्रूत ॥ ऋत्विग्निः कृतं कर्म यजमानोऽपि कृतवान्भवतीति यदिदमिंद्रविषयं वचो धृतान्मधुनश्च स्वाडुतरं भवत्वित्याशास्त्रे । तदाह भगवानाश्वलायनः ।
> वच एव म इदं धृतान्मधुनश्च स्वादीयोऽस्ति प्रीतिः स्वादीयोऽस्त्वितेव तदाह ।
> आ. गृ. १-१.५ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O priests, to अगोरुधाय (*agorudhāya*): one who obstructs praises is a गोरुधः (*gorudhaḥ*); one who is not such is an agorudh; the meaning is, he does not destroy them but hears them with respect. To such an Indra, and therefore to one who desires praises (गविषे (*gaviṣe*)), to the shining (द्युक्षाय (*dyukṣāya*)) Indra, speak (वोचत (*vocata*), that is, utter) a speech in the form of praise that is beautiful (दस्म्यं (*dasmyaṃ*)), sweeter than ghee (धृतात् (*dhṛtāt*)), which is sweeter than clarified butter, and than honey (मधुनश्च (*madhunaśca*)): exceedingly sweet (स्वादीयः (*svādīyaḥ*)). A rite done by the priests is also done by the sacrificer; so the wish is that this speech concerning Indra may be sweeter than ghee and honey. The venerable Āśvalāyana says this: वच एव म इदं धृतान्मधुनश्च स्वादीयोऽस्ति प्रीतिः स्वादीयोऽस्त्वितेव तदाह । (*vaca eva ma idaṃ dhṛtānmadhunaśca svādīyo 'sti prītiḥ svādīyo 'stviteva tadāha |*) (Ā. Gṛ. 1-1.5) **GLOSS** **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अगोरुधाय (*agorudhāya*)—(O priests) one who does not reject praises
- गविषे (*gaviṣe*)—one who desires praise alone
- द्युक्षाय (*dyukṣāya*)—and is luminous, with Indra as the object
- दस्म्यं (*dasmyaṃ*)—that which is attractive
### Printed p. 94
- घृतात् (*ghṛtāt*)—than ghee
- मधुनश्च (*madhunaśca*)—and than honey
- स्वादीयः (*svādīyaḥ*)—that which satisfies by a greater flavour
- वाचः (*vācaḥ*)—the speech of praise
- वोचत (*vocata*)—recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, to Indra, who does not reject praises but desires them, and is luminous, recite a speech of praise that is attractive, and satisfying by a flavour greater than that of ghee and than that of honey.

##### English translation
Recite agreeable words, sweeter than clarified butter or than Soma, to the illustrious (Indra), who is gratified by eulogy, who rejects not praise.
#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्यामि॑तानि वी॒र्या॒३॒॑ ३ न राधः॒ पर्ये॑तवे ।  
> ज्योति॒र्न विश्व॑म॒भ्यस्ति॒ दक्षि॑णा ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यस्य । अमितानि । वीर्या । न । राधः । परिएतवे ।  
> ज्योतिः । न । विश्वं । अभि । अस्ति । दक्षिणा ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्येंद्रस्य वीर्या वीर्याणि वृत्रहननादिलक्षणानि सामर्थ्य्यन्यमितानि
> अस्येयंति सामार्थ्यानि नान्यानीति परिमितानि न भवंति । यद्वा ॥ मीज् हिंसायां ।
> छांदसो ह्रस्वः ॥ शत्रुभिरहिंसितानि भवंति । तथा यस्येंद्रस्य राधो धनं पर्येतवे शत्रुभिः परिगंतुं प्राप्तुं शक्यं न भवति । अत एव यस्य दक्षिणा धनं दानं
> विश्वमभ्यस्ति सर्वं स्तोतृजनमभिभवति । तत्र दृष्टांतः । ज्योतिर्न । ज्योतिषामयनत्वाज्योतिरंतरिक्षं । यथांतरिक्षं सर्वलोकं सिधाय तिष्ठति तद्वत्सोतृजनं
> धनदानेन पिधत्त इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The deeds of valour (वीर्या (*vīryā*)) of that Indra, marked by the slaying of Vṛtra and the like, are immeasurable (अमितानि (*amitāni*)): such capacities of his are not limited, as though they were only so much and no more. Or else, the root meaning "to harm" gives a sense of unharmed: they are not harmed by enemies. Likewise the wealth (राधः (*rādhaḥ*)) of that Indra cannot be gone around or reached by enemies (पर्येतवे (*paryetave*)). For that very reason his gift (दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)), the giving of wealth, overcomes (अभ्यस्ति (*abhyasti*)) the whole body of reciters. The example is ज्योतिर्न (*jyotirna*): the sky is the home of the luminaries; as the sky stays covering all the worlds, so he covers the body of reciters with gifts of wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Indra
- वीर्या (*vīryā*)—the deeds of valour such as the slaying of Vṛtra
- अमितानि (*amitāni*)—are immeasurable (are unobstructed)
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—the wealth of which Indra
- पर्येतवे (*paryetave*)—
### Printed p. 95
cannot be carried off by enemies
- न (*na*)—it is not possible
- दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)—the giving of wealth of which Indra
- ज्योतिर्न (*jyotirna*)—like the sky
- विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)—all
- अभ्यस्ति (*abhyasti*)—covers: praise that Indra

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Praise that Indra whose deeds, such as the slaying of Vṛtra, are immeasurable and unobstructed, whose wealth cannot be carried off by enemies, and whose giving of wealth covers all, like the sky.

##### English translation
Whose energies are unbounded; whose wealth cannot be carried away ; whose bounty extends like the firmament over all.
#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स्तु॒हींद्रं॑ व्यश्व॒वदनू॑र्मिं वा॒जिनं॒ यमं॑ ।  
> अ॒र्यो गयं॒ मंह॑मानं॒ वि दा॒शुषे॑ ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> स्तुहि । इंद्रं । व्यश्ववत् । अनूर्मिं । वाजिनं । यमं ।  
> अर्यः । गयं । मंहमानं । वि । दाशुषे ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विश्वमनः अनूर्मिं । ऊर्मिर्हिंसाकर्मा । कैश्चिदप्यहिंस्यं । अथवा
> शत्रुभिरगंतव्यं । अत एव वाजिनं बलवंतं यमं स्तोतृभिः सुनियतमेता दृश्यमिंद्रं स्तुहि । स्तोत्रे दृष्टांतः । व्यश्ववत् । यथा व्यश्नो विश्वमनसः पितेंद्रमस्तोत्तद्वत्सु हीत्यर्थः । स्तुतश्चेत् आर्यः स्वामींद्रॆ दाशुषे हविर्दत्तवते
> यजमानाय मंहमानं पूज्यमानं गयं धनं । यद्वा । देवानां पूजाय्कॆ
> गयं गृहं । गृहमस्ति चेत् देवा हविर्भिः पूज्यंते । तादृशं गृहं वितरति ।
> तस्मात्त्वं धनगृहलाभाय स्तुहीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Viśvamanas, praise Indra, who is अनूर्मिं (*anūrmiṃ*): the word ūrmi has the sense of harming, so he is one who cannot be harmed by anyone, or whom enemies cannot approach. For that very reason he is powerful (वाजिनं (*vājinaṃ*)), well restrained (यमं (*yamaṃ*)) by the reciters, and visible. The example for the praise is व्यश्ववत् (*vyaśvavat*): as Vyaśva, the father of Viśvamanas, praised Indra, so praise him. If praised, the lord Indra gives to the sacrificer who has given oblation honoured (मंहमानं (*maṃhamānaṃ*)) wealth (गयं (*gayaṃ*)). Or else, गयं (*gayaṃ*) is a house for the worship of the gods: if there is a house in which the gods are worshipped with oblations, he bestows such a house. Therefore praise him for the gain of wealth and a house.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अनूर्मिं (*anūrmiṃ*)—(O sage Viśvamanas) one who is unharmed by anyone
- वाजिनं (*vājinaṃ*)—endowed with power
- यमं (*yamaṃ*)—the cause of the restraint of the reciters (or one restrained by the praise of reciters)
### Printed p. 96
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- व्यश्ववत् (*vyaśvavat*)—as your father Vyaśva praised
- स्तुहि (*stuhi*)—praise
- आर्यः (*āryaḥ*)—the venerable Indra
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the sacrificer who offers oblation
- मंहमानं (*maṃhamānaṃ*)—that which is worthy of honour
- गयं (*gayaṃ*)—wealth or a house
- वि (*vi*)—he gives abundantly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sage Viśvamanas, praise Indra, who is unharmed by anyone, endowed with power, the cause of the restraint of the reciters or one restrained by the praises of the reciters, as your father Vyaśva praised him. The venerable Indra gives abundantly to the sacrificer who offers oblation exceedingly precious wealth or a house.

##### English translation
Glorify Indra, the unassailable, the powerful, the regulator (of men) as was done by Vyashwa ; he, the lord, gives a spacious dwelling to the donor (of the oblation).
#### Ṛk 23

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा नू॒नमुप॑ स्तुहि॒ वैय॑श्व दश॒मं नवं॑ ।  
> सुवि॑द्वांसं च॒र्कृत्यं॑ च॒रणी॑नां ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । नूनं । उप । स्तुहि । वैयश्व । दशमं । नवं ।  
> सुविद्वांसं । चर्कृत्यं । चरणीनां ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वैयश्व व्यश्वस्य पुत्र विश्वमनः चरणीनां मनुष्याणां देहे स्थितानां
> नवानां प्राणानां दशमं दशसंख्यापूरकं । तत्र मंत्रः । नव वै पुरुषे प्राणा
> मनुष्येषु वर्तमाना इंद्रस्तेषां दशधा भवतींद्रस्यात्मानं दशधा चरंतमिति ।
> एतादृशं आत एव नवं स्तुत्यं सुविद्वांसमंतर्यामित्वात् सुष्टु सर्वं जानंतं
> चर्कृत्यं भूयो भूयः कार्येषु सर्वैर्नमस्कर्तव्यं एवंविधमिंद्रमेव
> नूनविदानीमुप स्तुहि । समीपे स्तुहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vaiyaśva, son of Vyaśva, Viśvamanas: Indra is the tenth (दशमं (*daśamaṃ*)), the completer of the number ten, of the nine vital breaths dwelling in the body of men (चरणीनां (*caraṇīnāṃ*)). On this there is a mantra: नव वै पुरुषे प्राणा मनुष्येषु वर्तमाना इंद्रस्तेषां दशधा भवतींद्रस्यात्मानं दशधा चरंतमिति । (*nava vai puruṣe prāṇā manuṣyeṣu vartamānā iṃdrasteṣāṃ daśadhā bhavatīṃdrasyātmānaṃ daśadhā caraṃtamiti |*) **GLOSS** Such, and therefore praiseworthy (नवं (*navaṃ*)), as inner controller knowing all well (सुविद्वांसं (*suvidvāṃsaṃ*)), to be saluted by all again and again in rites (चर्कृत्यं (*carkṛtyaṃ*)): praise such an Indra alone now, near at hand.
### Printed p. 97

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वैयत्व (*vaiyatva*)—O sage named Viśvamanas, son of Vyaśva
- चरणीनां (*caraṇīnāṃ*)—of men, who completes with the nine vital breaths within the body
- दशमं (*daśamaṃ*)—who is of the form of the tenth breath
- नवं (*navaṃ*)—the praiseworthy
- सुविद्वांसं (*suvidvāṃsaṃ*)—who knows everything as the inner controller
- चर्कृत्यं एव (*carkṛtyaṃ eva*)—who is saluted by all again and again, Indra himself
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- उप स्तुहि (*upa stuhi*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sage named Viśvamanas, son of Vyaśva: praise now Indra himself, who is of the form of the tenth breath that completes the nine breaths within the body of men, who is praiseworthy, knows everything as the inner controller, and is saluted by all again and again.

##### English translation
Praise verily at present, son of Vyshwa, praise (Indra), who is the tenth of the pervading (vital principles), the adorable, the all-wise, to be honoured repeatedly (by sacred rites).
#### Ṛk 24

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वेत्था॒ हि निर्ऋ॑तीनां॒ वज्र॑हस्त परि॒वृजं॑ ।  
> अह॑रहः शुं॒ध्युः प॑रि॒पदा॑मिव ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वेत्थ । हि । निःयतीनां । वज्रहस्त । परिवृजं ।  
> अहः अहः । शुंध्युः । परिपदां इव ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इदानीमृषिरिंद्रं संबोध्याह । हे वज्रहस्त वज्रयुक्तहस्तेंद्र निर्युतीनामुपद्रवकारिणां रक्षसां परिवृजं परिवर्जनं । हिरवधारणे । त्वमेव वेत्थ ।
> जानीषे । तत्र दृष्टांतः । अहरहः शुंध्युः । अस्मिन्नुदिते सति ब्राह्मणा आत्मीयं
> कर्म कृत्वा शुद्धा भवंतीति शोधनहेतुत्वात् शुंध्युरादित्यः । परिपदामिव परितो
> यजमानानामिव । यद्वा। परिपदां समानाधिकरणः । परितः पततां पक्षीणां वर्जनं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now the seer addresses Indra. O Indra, thunderbolt-handed (वज्रहस्त (*vajrahasta*)), with a hand armed with the thunderbolt: you alone know (वेत्थ (*vettha*)), that is, understand, how to avoid (परिवृजं (*parivṛjaṃ*)) the troublesome rākṣasas, the nirṛtis. The example is अहरहः शुंध्युः (*aharahaḥ śuṃdhyuḥ*): when he has risen, the brāhmaṇas, having performed their own rites, become pure; because he is the cause of purification, the sun is the purifier (शुंध्युः (*śuṃdhyuḥ*)). परिपदामिव (*paripadāmiva*): as around the sacrificers. Or else, परिपदां (*paripadāṃ*) stands in the same case-relation: the avoidance of the birds that fall all around

### Printed p. 98

> स्वस्थानत्यागमहरहः प्रतिदिवसं यथा वेत्ति । उदिते सूर्ये पक्षिणः स्वस्थानं
> परित्यज्य सर्वतो गच्छंति खलु । एवं त्वयांद्रे स्वबलेन प्रकाशमाने सति शत्रवः
> स्वपुरादि त्यक्त्वा पलायंत इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa continues: just as day by day the sun knows the leaving of their own place, so, when the sun has risen, the birds abandon their own place and go off in every direction. In the same way, Indra, when you shine with your own strength, the enemies leave their own cities and flee. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वज्रहस्त (*vajrahasta*)—O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt weapon
- अहरहः (*aharahaḥ*)—every day
- शुंध्युः (*śuṃdhyuḥ*)—the sun
- परिपदां इव (*paripadāṃ iva*)—as one knows the movements of the birds that run to the various quarters
- निर्यतीनां (*niryatīnāṃ*)—the asuras, who are a trouble to the people
- परिवृजं (*parivṛjaṃ*)—their running away, abandoning their own cities for fear of you
- वेत्थ हि (*vettha hi*)—you know indeed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt weapon, as the sun knows every day, on rising, the movements of the birds that run to the various quarters, so you know how the asuras, who are a trouble to the people, abandon their own cities for fear of you and run away.

##### English translation
You are cognizant, wielder of the thunderbolt, of the departure of evil beings, as the purifying sun day by day (is of that) of the (birds) flying in all directions (from their roost).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

On परिपदां इव (*paripadāṃ iva*) the sense "of the sacrificers" is also given: the sacrificers who rise every day and, intent on their own rites, go about in various places.

#### Ṛk 25

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तदिं॒द्राव॒ आ भ॑र॒ येना॑ दंसिष्ठ॒ कृत्व॑ने ।  
> द्वि॒ता कुत्सा॑य शिश्नथो॒ नि चो॑दय ॥ २५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तत् । इंद्र । अवः । आ । भर । येन । दंसिष्ठ । कृत्वने ।  
> द्विता । कुत्साय । शिश्नथः । नि । जोदय ॥ २५ ॥

### Printed p. 99

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र तदवस्तद्रक्षणमस्मभ्यमा भर । हे दंसिष्ठात्यंतं दर्शनीय यद्वा
> शत्रूणामुपक्षपयितरिंद्र कृत्वने कर्म कुर्वते यजमानाय तदर्थं येन
> पालनमकृथाः तद्रक्षणमा भरेति समन्वयः । किंच कुत्साय कुत्सनामकाय
> राजर्षये द्विता द्विधा द्विप्रकारेण शिश्नथः त्वं शत्रूनवधीः । तस्मै द्वैधं पालनमकार्षीरित्यर्थः । तद्रक्षणमस्मभ्यं नि चोदय । नितरामत्यर्थं प्रेरय । यद्वा ।
> कृत्वन इति सामान्येनोक्त्वा निःशेषेण तदेवाह कुत्सायेत्यादि । शेषं पूर्ववत्॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, bring to us that protection. O Dasiṣṭha, exceedingly beautiful one, or else, destroyer of enemies, Indra: for the sacrificer who performs the rite (कृत्वने (*kṛtvane*)), bring that protection with which you gave him protection; this is the construction. Further, for Kutsa, the royal seer of that name, you slew the enemies in two ways (द्विता (*dvitā*), twofold), that is, you gave him protection of two kinds. Impel that protection to us, urge it on exceedingly. Or else: having first said कृत्वन (*kṛtvana*) in general terms, he then specifies it in full with "for Kutsa" and what follows. The rest as before.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दंसिष्ठ (*daṃsiṣṭha*)—you of beautiful form
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- येन (*yena*)—by which act of protection
- कुत्साय (*kutsāya*)—for the protection of Kutsa
- द्विता (*dvitā*)—in two ways
- शिश्नथः (*śiśnathaḥ*)—you slew the enemy
- तत् अवः (*tat avaḥ*)—that same protection
- कृत्वने (*kṛtvane*)—to the sacrificer, the performer of the rite also
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—provide
- नि चोदय (*ni codaya*)—impel it (to us also)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of beautiful form, with the act of protection by which, for the protection of Kutsa, you slew the enemy in two ways, provide that same protection to the sacrificer, the performer of the rite, also. Impel it to us also.

##### English translation
Indra, of goodly aspect, bring to the offerer (of the oblation) that (protection) wherewith to defend him you have twice slain (the foe) for Kutsa ; show the same (care of us).

#### Ṛk 26

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तमु॑ त्वा नू॒नमी॑महे॒ नव्यं॑ दंसिष्ठ॒ सन्य॑से ।  
> स त्वं नो॒ विश्वा॑ अ॒भिमा॑तीः स॒क्षणिः॑ ॥ २६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । ऊं इति । त्वा । नूनं । ईमहे । नव्यं । दंसिष्ठ । सन्यसे ।  
> सः । त्वं । नः । विश्वाः । अभिमातीः । सक्षणिः ॥ २६ ॥

### Printed p. 100

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे दंसिष्ठातिशयेन दर्शनीयेंद्र नव्यं स्तोतृभिः स्तोतृव्यं तमु तादृशमेव त्वा त्वां नूनमिदानीमीमहे । वयं याचामहे । किमर्थं । संन्यसे ॥
> असु क्षेपणे । भावे क्विप् ॥ संन्यासार्थं याचामह इति शेषः । स ताद्रशस्त्वं
> नोऽस्माकं विश्वाः सर्वा अभिमातीः शत्रुसेनाः सक्षणिः ॥ सहेः सनिप्रत्ययः ॥
> सहनशीलोऽभिभवनशीलो भवसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Dasiṣṭha, Indra, exceedingly beautiful, you who are to be praised by the praisers (नव्यं (*navyaṃ*)), just such a one as you we now implore; we beg. For what purpose? For संन्यसे (*saṃnyase*); the sense is "we beg for the sake of संन्यासार्थं (*saṃnyāsārthaṃ*)", this being the word to be supplied. Such as you are, you are the one who is of a patient and overcoming nature towards all our hostile armies. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दंसिष्ठ (*daṃsiṣṭha*)—O Indra, of exceedingly beautiful form
- नव्यं (*navyaṃ*)—worthy of the praise of the praisers
- तमु (*tamu*)—and renowned
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you alone
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- संन्यसे (*saṃnyase*)—for our well-being
- ईमहे (*īmahe*)—we beg
- सः (*saḥ*)—such a renowned one
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- विश्वाः (*viśvāḥ*)—all
- अभिमातीः (*abhimātīḥ*)—hostile armies
- सक्षणिः (*sakṣaṇiḥ*)—you become the one who defeats

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of exceedingly beautiful form, you who are worthy of the praise of the praisers and renowned, you alone we now beg for our well-being. Such a renowned one as you become the defeater of all our hostile armies.

##### English translation
(Indra) of goodly aspect, we implore you who are entitled to praise, for (our) preservation ; for you are the overcomer of all our adversaries.

#### Ṛk 27

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य ऋक्षा॒दंह॑सो मु॒चद्यो वार्या॑त्स॒प्त सिंधु॑षु ।  
> वध॑र्दा॒सस्य॑ तुविनृष्ण नीनमः ॥ २७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । युक्षात् । अ०हसः । मुचत् । यः । वा । आर्यात् । सप्त । सिंधुषु ।  
> वधः । दासस्य । तुविन्मृ । नीनमः ॥ २७ ॥

### Printed p. 101

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वर्धचः परोक्षकृतः । य इंद्र ऋत्वात् ॥ यून् मनुष्यान्
> क्षणोति । क्षणोतेर्णादिको दप्रत्ययः ॥ तस्माद्रक्षसो जातादंहसः पापरूपादुपद्रवान्मुचत् मुंचति । राक्षस एनं न बाधते किं पुनस्तं हंतीत्यर्थः । अपि च य
> इंद्रः सप्त सिंधुषु गंगाद्यासु नदीषु । यद्वा । सप्त सर्पणशीलासु सिंधुषु ।
> तत्कूलेष्टित्यर्थः । गंगायां घोष इतिवत् । पा. १-४-४२ । तेषु वर्तमानानां
> स्तोतृणामार्यात् धनादिकं प्रेरयेत् ॥ ऋ गतिप्रापणयोः । आशीर्लिजि
> गुणोर्तिसंयोगाद्योः । पा. ७-४-२९ । इति गुणः । बहुलं छंदसीति
> लिज्यप्यादागमः ॥ अथ प्रत्यक्षः । हे तुविनृम्म बहुधनेंद्र दासस्योपक्षपयितुरसुरस्य
> वधर्हननसाधकमायुधं नीनमः । नमय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The first half of the Ṛk is in the third person (परोक्षकृतः (*parokṣakṛtaḥ*)). Indra is the one who frees from the affliction, the sin, born of the rākṣasa (the sense of यक्षात् (*yakṣāt*) is given as the harm-doer of men): the rākṣasa does not trouble him, much less does he kill him; this is the meaning. Further, Indra is the one who, in the seven rivers, the Gaṅgā and the rest, or else in the seven flowing rivers, that is, on their banks, sends wealth and the like to the praisers who dwell there. Then comes the direct address: O Tuvinṛmṇa, Indra of much wealth, bend, that is, hurl, the weapon, the instrument of killing, against the Dāsa, the asura who harms. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- यक्षात् (*yakṣāt*)—arisen from the rākṣasas who are the harmers of men
- अंहसः (*aṃhasaḥ*)—from sin
- मुचत् (*mucat*)—frees the sacrificers
- यः वा (*yaḥ vā*)—who
- सप्त (*sapta*)—seven in number
- सिंधुषु (*siṃdhuṣu*)—in the rivers, the Gaṅgā and the rest
- आर्यात् (*āryāt*)—sends many kinds of wealth (he is Indra indeed)
- तुविनृष्ण (*tuvinṛṣṇa*)—O Indra, possessor of many kinds of wealth
- दासस्य (*dāsasya*)—of the harming asura
- वधः (*vadhaḥ*)—the weapon, the instrument of killing
- नीनमः (*nīnamaḥ*)—apply

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

He who frees the sacrificers from the sin arisen from the rākṣasas who harm men, he who sends many kinds of wealth in the seven rivers, the Gaṅgā and the rest, is Indra indeed. O Indra, possessor of many kinds of wealth, apply against him the weapon, the instrument of killing, of the harming asura.

##### English translation
(He it is) who rescues men from the wickedness of evil beings, who enriches (the dwellers) on the seven rivers: now hurl, you who abound in wealth, your weapon at the Dasa.

#### Ṛk 28

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यथा॑ वरो सु॒षाम्णे॑ स॒निभ्य॒ आ व॑हो र॒यिं ।  
> व्य॑श्वेभ्यः सुभगे वाजिनीवति ॥ २८ ॥

### Printed p. 102

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यथा । वरो इति । सुसाम्ने । सनिभ्यः । आ । अवहः । रयिं ।  
> विअश्वेभ्यः । सुभगे । वाजिनीववति ॥ २८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनेन तृचेन वरोर्दानं स्तूयते । हे वरो वरुनामक राजन् सुषाम्न्य
> सुसाम्मे सुषमाख्यं राजानं स्वपितरमुद्दिश्य तस्योत्तमलोकप्राप्त्यर्थं सनिभ्यो
> भिक्षमाणेभ्य आ कोशादाहृत्य रयिं धनं यथा पुरावहः प्रापितवानसि अत
> एवविदानीं व्यश्वेभ्यो व्यश्वपुत्रेभ्योऽस्मभ्यं धनमावह । वाजिनीवतीति पदलिंगादियमुषस्या । अयं तृचोप्युषस्य इति शौनकेनोक्तं । यथा वरो सुषाम्न्य
> इत्युत्तमसौषसस्त्र च इति । हे सुभगे शोभनधनयुक्ते वाजनीवत्यन्नवति ॥
> मतुबनुवादार्थः ॥ यद्वा । वाजो वाजनं गमनमस्यास्तीति वाजिन्यन्नं । तद्वति
> हे उषः त्वं चास्मभ्यं धनं प्रयच्छ । वरोर्बहुधनदानात्तस्य दानस्तुतिः । यद्वा ।
> विश्वमना ऋषिर्वरुं संबोध्याह । हे सुभगे शोभनधने वाजिनीवत्यन्नवति
> हे उषः यथा त्वं सुषाम्न्य सुषामनाम्मॆ मम पित्रे धनं दत्वा तेनैव सुषाम्न्य
> सनिभ्यो याचमानेभ्यो धनं यथा प्रापितवत्यसि तेन यथा दानमकारयः एवं
> मह्यमपि धनं दत्वा व्यश्वेभ्यः ॥ पूजायां बहुवचनं ॥ व्यश्वपुत्राय विश्वमनसे
> धनं प्रापयेति मयापि दानं करोषि । हे वरो उपसमेवं वदेत्यृषिराह ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By this group of three Ṛks the generosity of Varu is praised. O Varu, king so named: for Suṣāman, the king of that name, your own father, for the winning of the highest world for him, you formerly brought wealth taken from your treasury to the petitioners (सनिभ्यो (*sanibhyo*)). So now bring wealth to us, the sons of Vyaśva (व्यश्वेभ्यो (*vyaśvebhyo*)). From the word वाजिनीवति (*vājinīvati*), which has the feminine form, this Ṛk is addressed to Uṣas. Śaunaka also says that this group of three is addressed to Uṣas, and that the Ṛk "yathā varo suṣāmṇye" belongs to the Uttama Sauṣasa Sūkta. O Subhagā, you who possess goodly wealth, O Vājinīvatī, possessor of food: the suffix is restating what is already known. Or else: वाज (*vāja*) is going, and वाजिन्यन्नं (*vājinyannaṃ*) is food, so, O Uṣas, possessor of that, give us wealth as well. Because Varu gave much wealth, his generosity is praised. Or else: the seer Viśvamanas, addressing Varu, says: O Subhagā, Vājinīvatī, Uṣas, as you gave wealth to my father Suṣāman, and by that wealth had gifts made to the petitioners, so now give wealth to me also, to the Vyaśvas (the plural is one of respect), the son of Vyaśva, Viśvamanas, and so bring about gifts through me also. O Varu, speak thus to Uṣas, the seer says. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरो (*varo*)—O king named Varu
- सुषाम्णे (*suṣāmṇe*)—for your father named Suṣāman, for the attainment of the highest world
- स निभ्यः (*sa nibhyaḥ*)—to petitioners
- आ (*ā*)—taking from your treasury
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- यथा (*yathā*)—in what manner (formerly)
- अवहः (*avahaḥ*)—you gave (so also)
- व्यश्वभ्यः (*vyaśvabhyaḥ*)—to us also, the sons of Vyaśva, now give
- सुभगे (*subhage*)—you who have goodly wealth
- वाजिनीवति (*vājinīvati*)—you who have food, O Uṣas (you also give wealth to us)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O king named Varu, as you formerly gave wealth, taken from your treasury, to petitioners for the attainment of the highest world for your father Suṣāman, so now give to us also, the son of Vyaśva. O Uṣas, possessed of goodly wealth and of food, you also give wealth to us.

### Printed p. 103

##### English translation
As you, Varu, have distributed vast wealth to those who have solicited triches) on behalf of Sushaman. (so do you now distribute) to the descendants of Vyshwa: (and so too you), auspicious food-bestowing (Ushas). **NOTE**

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Addressing वरो (*varo*) as a king named Varuṇa, the commentator says that another meaning is also possible: "O Varuṇa, you pray to Uṣas. As Uṣas formerly provided wealth to Suṣāman and through him had wealth given to all the petitioners, so now have it given to me also; pray thus on our behalf." **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 29

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ ना॒र्यस्य॒ दक्षि॑णा॒ व्य॑श्वा एतु सो॒मिनः॑ ।  
> स्थू॒रं च॒ राधः॑ श॒तव॑त्स॒हस्र॑वत् ॥ २९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नार्यस्य । दक्षिणा । विआश्वान् । एतु । सोमिनः ।  
> स्थूरं । च । राधः । शतवत् । सहस्रवत् ॥ २९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आनया धनमृषिरादत्तवानित्याह । नार्यस्य । नरहितो नर्यः । तस्यापत्यं
> नार्यः ॥ तस्मात्संबंधमात्रे तस्येदमित्यण् ॥ तस्य सोमिनः सोमवतो
> यजमानस्य । यद्वा । व्यश्वानां विशेषणं । तादृशस्य वरोर्दक्षिणा दानं
> सोमवतो व्यश्वान् व्यश्वपुत्रानस्मान्नैतु । आगच्छतु । किंच स्फूरं स्कूलं शतवत्सहस्रवच्छतसहस्रधनयुक्तं राधोऽन्नं चास्मानागच्छतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By this Ṛk the seer says that he has received the wealth. नार्यस्य (*nāryasya*): one who is good for men is नर्यः (*naryaḥ*), his descendant is नार्यः (*nāryaḥ*); the aṇ affix is used for a mere relationship. Of him, the possessor of soma, the sacrificer; or else, this is an epithet of the Vyaśvas. May the gift (दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)) of such a Varu come to us, the sons of Vyaśva, who are possessors of soma. Further, may abundant wealth (राधः (*rādhaḥ*)), that is food, endowed with hundreds and thousands, come to us as well.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नार्यस्य (*nāryasya*)—of Varu, the son of Narya, who is good for men
- दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)—the gift
- सोमिनः (*sominaḥ*)—the sacrificers who offer soma
- व्यश्वान् (*vyaśvān*)—us, the son of Vyaśva
- अएतु (*aetu*)—may it come and join
- स्फूरं (*sphūraṃ*)—abundant
- शतवत् (*śatavat*)—numbering hundreds
- सहस्रवत् (*sahasravat*)—numbering thousands
- राधः चॆ (*rādhaḥ cè*)—food and wealth also, may they come and join us

### Printed p. 104

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May the gift given by Varu, the son of Narya who is good for men, come and join us, the sacrificers who offer soma and the sons of Vyaśva. May food and wealth also, abundant, numbering hundreds and numbering thousands, come and join us.

##### English translation
May the gifts of a humane (prince), when offering the soma libation extend to the Vyshwas, and substantial wealth by hundreds and thousand.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

सोमिनः (*sominaḥ*) may also be construed as an epithet of Varu, so that the sense is "the gift of Varu, who is himself the sacrificer".

#### Ṛk 30

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यत्त्वा॑ पृ॒च्छादी॑जा॒नः कु॑ह॒या कु॑हयाकृते ।  
> ए॒षो अप॑श्रितो व॒लो गो॑म॒तीमव॑ तिष्ठति ॥ ३० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । त्वा । पृच्छात् । ईजानः । कुहया । कुहयाकृते ।  
> एषः । अपश्रितः । वलः । गोऽमतीं । अव । शिष्ठति ॥ ३० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इदानीमुषसं संबोध्याभिधीयते । हे कुहयाकृते स वरुः कुह
> कुत्र तिष्ठतीतेतदिच्छयाभिलक्षणप्रवृत्तैर्जिज्ञासुभिः पुरस्कृते ॥ कुहशब्दात्क्यच् ॥
> एतादृशे हे उषः त्वा त्वां यद्यदा कश्चित्पृच्छात् पृच्छति ईजान इष्टवान् वरुः
> कुहया क्व तिष्ठतीति यदा पृच्छति तदानीमपश्रितः सर्वैराश्रितः । यद्वा । विवृतद्वारः ।
> यदा याचमाना आगच्छंति तदा दौवारिका न प्रतिबध्नंतीत्यर्थः । तादृशो बलो
> वरः स्वबलेनावारकः शत्रूणां । यद्वा । भिक्षूणां धनादिप्रदानेनावरिता। एषो एष
> वरुर्गॊमतीमेतन्नामिकां नदीं ॥ कालाध्वनोरिति द्वितीया ॥ तस्तास्तीरेव
> तिष्यतीति तदानीं त्वं कथयसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now Uṣas is addressed. O कुहयाकृते (*kuhayākṛte*), you who are put forward, that is, questioned, by inquirers who set out with the wish to know "Where does that Varu stay?" (the word is formed from कुह (*kuha*), "where"). O Uṣas, when anyone asks you where the sacrificer Varu stays, then you say as follows. He is resorted to by all (अपश्रितः (*apaśritaḥ*)); or else, he has open doors: when petitioners come, the doorkeepers do not obstruct them. Such a Varu averts enemies by his own strength; or else, he is one who keeps the beggars from want by giving wealth and the like. This Varu stays on the very banks of the river named Gomatī.

### Printed p. 105

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कुहयाकृते (*kuhayākṛte*)—O Uṣas, who are questioned about Varu as to where he is
- ईजानः (*ījānaḥ*)—Varu, the sacrificer
- कुहया (*kuhayā*)—where he is
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- पृच्छात् (*pṛcchāt*)—one asks, then
- अपश्रितः (*apaśritaḥ*)—the refuge of all
- वलः (*valaḥ*)—and one who faces enemies
- एषः (*eṣaḥ*)—this Varu
- गोमतीं (*gomatīṃ*)—on the bank of the river Gomatī
- अव तिष्ठति (*ava tiṣṭhati*)—is staying (say so)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Uṣas, who are questioned about Varu as to where he is: if anyone asks you, "Where is Varu, the sacrificer?", then tell him, "Varu, the refuge of all, is on the bank of the river Gomatī."

##### English translation
If any ask of you, (Ushas), when anywhere present, where the sacrificer (Varu dwells), (reply) the powerfull (prince), the refuge of all, abides on (the banks of) the Gomathi river.

## Sūkta 25 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ता वामिति चतुर्विंशत्यृचं पंचमं सूक्तं । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका । ता
> वां चतुर्विंशतिर्मृत्रावरुणं दशम्याद्यास्तिस्रो वैश्वदेव्य उपांत्योष्टिग्र्भेति ।
> व्यश्वपुत्रो विश्वमना ऋषिः । उष्णिक् छंदः । उपांत्योष्टिग्र्भा षट्सप्तैकादशा उष्णिग्र्भा । अनु. ४-३ । इति तल्लक्षणोपेतत्वात् । दशम्येकादशीद्वादश्यो
> वैश्वदेव्योऽवशिष्टानां मित्रावरुणौ देवता ॥ सूक्तविनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning ता वां (*tā vāṃ*) has twenty-four Ṛks and is the fifth sūkta. On it he cites the Anukramaṇikā: the twenty-four Ṛks are addressed to Mitrāvaruṇa, the three beginning with the tenth are Vaiśvadevī, and the last but one is of the uṣṇig-garbhā metre. The seer is Viśvamanas, the son of Vyaśva; the metre is uṣṇik. The last-but-one Ṛk is uṣṇig-garbhā because it has the marks "six, seven, eleven" (Anu. 4-3). The tenth, eleventh and twelfth are Vaiśvadevī; Mitrāvaruṇa is the deity of the rest. The use of the sūkta is by indication (लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*)). **NOTE**

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta, beginning ता वां (*tā vāṃ*), is the fifth sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. It has twenty-four Ṛks. The Anukramaṇikā is quoted to the effect that the twenty-four Ṛks are addressed to Mitrāvaruṇa, with the three beginning with the tenth to the Viśvedevas, and that the last but one is of the uṣṇig-garbhā metre. The seer of this sūkta is Viśvamanas, the son of Vyaśva. The deity of the tenth, eleventh and twelfth Ṛks is the Viśvedevas; the deity of the remaining Ṛks is Mitrāvaruṇa; the metre is uṣṇik.

### Printed p. 106

The Ṛk before the last, that is, the twenty-third Ṛk, is of the metre called uṣṇig-garbhā. If a Ṛk has three pādas, with six syllables in the first pāda, seven in the second and eleven in the third, that Ṛk is of the metre called uṣṇig-garbhā. The use of the sūkta is by indication (laiṅgika).

##### Particulars

Sūkta 25. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 4, Sūkta 25. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Vargas 21, 22, 23, 24, 25. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 24. Seer: Viśvamanas Vaiyaśva. Deity: 1-9 and 13-24 Mitrāvaruṇau; 10-12 Viśvedevāḥ. Metre: 1-22 and 24 Uṣṇik; 23 Uṣṇig-garbhā.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ता वां॒ विश्व॑स्य गो॒पा दे॒वा दे॒वेषु॑ य॒ज्ञिया॑ ।  
> ऋ॒तावा॑ना यजसे पू॒तद॑क्षसा ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ता । वां । विश्वस्य । गोपा । देवा । देवेषु । यज्जिया ।  
> ऋतऽवाना । यजसे । पूतदक्षसा ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मित्रावरुणौ विश्वस्य सर्वस्य लोकस्य गोपा गोपायितार् देवा देवौ
> द्योतनशीलौ देवेषु मध्यॆ यज्ञिया यज्ञार्हौ ता तौ तादृशौ वां युवां
> हविष्पदानार्थं यजमानं भजथः । आत एव हे विश्वमनः ऋतावानर्तावान्
> सत्यवंतौ यज्ञवंतौ वा पूतदक्षसौ शुद्धबलौ । आवां बलवंताविति वचनमात्रेण
> बलवंतौ न भवतः किंतु यथार्थत्वेन सामर्थ्यवंतौ । मित्रावरुणौ यजसे ।
> हविर्भिः पूजयसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Mitrāvaruṇa, guardians of the whole world, shining ones, worthy of sacrifice among the gods: such as you are, you attend the sacrificer so that he may offer the oblation. Therefore, O Viśvamanas, address them: they are truthful or possessed of sacrifice, and of pure strength. They are not strong merely by the saying "we are strong", but able in truth. You worship Mitrāvaruṇa with oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवा (*devā*)—the shining ones
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—among the gods
- यज्ञिया (*yajñiyā*)—worthy of worship
- ता (*tā*)—O renowned Mitrāvaruṇa
- वां (*vāṃ*)—you two alone
- विश्वस्य (*viśvasya*)—of all the worlds

### Printed p. 107

- गोपा (*gopā*)—you are the protectors (O seer Viśvamanas)
- यतावाना (*yatāvānā*)—the truthful
- पूतदक्षसा (*pūtadakṣasā*)—those of pure strength, Mitrāvaruṇa, to them alone
- यजसे (*yajase*)—worship, addressing yourself

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Mitrāvaruṇa, shining, worthy of worship among the gods, and renowned, you two alone are the protectors of all the worlds. O seer Viśvamanas, worship, addressing yourself to them, Mitrāvaruṇa, who are truthful and of pure strength.

##### English translation
You two are the protectors of the universe, divine, and to be adored among the gods; therefore, (Vyshwamanas), you sacrifice to the pair who are observant of truth and endowed with real power.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मि॒त्रा तना॒ न र॒थ्या॒३॒॑ ३ वरु॑णो॒ यश्च॑ सु॒क्रतुः॑ ।  
> स॒नात्सु॑जा॒ता तन॑या धृ॒तव्र॑ता ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मित्रा । तना । न । रथ्या । वरुणः । यः । च । सुक्रतुः ।  
> सनात् । सुजाता । तनया । धृतव्रता ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुक्रतुः शोभनकर्मा यो वरुणः सुकर्मा मित्रा च मित्रावरुणौ ।
> कीदृशौ । तना । तन्नंति मुकुटकटकादिनेति तनानि धनानि । नश्चार्थे । धनानि च रथ्या
> रथ्यौ नेतारौ । यद्वा । धनानि कर्मणः कर्तृपेक्षत्वात् प्रयच्छंताविति संबध्यते ।
> तादृशौ रथ्यौ रथवंतौ सनाच्चिरादेव सुजाता सुजात् शोभनजन्मानौ । तदेवाह ।
> तनया तनयावदितेः पुत्रौ धृतव्रता धृतव्रतौ धृतकर्माणौ ता यजस इति
> पूर्वेण समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Mitra, and Varuṇa who is of good action (सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*)): the two Mitrāvaruṇa. Of what kind? तना (*tanā*): that which is stretched, such as crowns and bracelets, that is, riches; the word na here stands for "and". They are the leaders (रथ्या (*rathyā*)) of riches; or else, since an act depends on its doer, the construction is that they bestow riches. Such possessors of chariots are, from of old, of good birth (सुजाता (*sujātā*)). He states the same: they are the sons of Aditi, upholders of vows and rites. The construction is with the earlier "you worship them".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*)—one of excellent action
- मित्रा चॆ (*mitrā cè*)—and the Mitra who has the same quality; these two, Mitrāvaruṇa
- तना (*tanā*)—

### Printed p. 108

  givers of wealth
- रथ्या (*rathyā*)—leaders of rites
- सनात् (*sanāt*)—from the beginning
- सुजाता (*sujātā*)—of excellent birth
- तनया (*tanayā*)—sons of Aditi
- धृतव्रता (*dhṛtavratā*)—those who impel the keeping of the vow by rule (worship such Mitrāvaruṇa)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Varuṇa who is of excellent action and the Mitra who has the same quality, these two, Mitrāvaruṇa, are givers of wealth, leaders of rites, of excellent birth from the beginning, sons of Aditi, and those who govern the vow by rule. Worship such Mitrāvaruṇa.

##### English translation
Mitra and Varuna, doers of good deeds, (diffusers of) riches, who are the charioteers (of men), well born of old, the sons (of Aditi) observant of vows, (you are worshipped by me).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ता मा॒ता वि॒श्ववे॑दसासु॒र्या॑य॒ प्रम॑हसा ।  
> म॒ही ज॑जा॒नादि॑तिर्ऋ॒ताव॑री ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ता ! माता । विश्ववेदसा । आसुर्याय । प्रमहसा ।  
> मही । जजान । अदितिः । ऋतवरी ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अदितेस्तनयत्वमेव स्पुटयति । विश्ववेदसा विश्ववेदसौ सर्वधना । यद्वा ।
> विश्वानि स्थावरजंगमात्मकानि सर्वाणि विदतुर्जानीत इति विश्ववेदसौ । प्रमहसा
> प्रमहसौ प्रकृष्टतेजस्कौ ता तौ तादृशौ मित्रावरुणौ मही महत्यृतावरी सत्यवती
> माता देवमातादितिर्जजान । जनयामास । किमर्थं । असुर्यायासुराणां
> हंत्रे बलाय । असुरान् हंतुमुत्पादितवतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He makes clear that they are Aditi's sons. विश्ववेदसा (*viśvavedasā*): those who possess all wealth; or else, those who know all things, moving and unmoving. प्रमहसा (*pramahasā*): of surpassing splendour. Such Mitrāvaruṇa were borne by Aditi, the great and truthful mother of the gods. For what purpose? असुर्याय (*asuryāya*): for the strength that slays the asuras; the meaning is that she bore them in order to slay the asuras.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्ववेदसा (*viśvavedasā*)—those who know the whole world of moving and unmoving things
- प्रमहसा (*pramahasā*)—those of the most excellent splendour
- शा (*śā*)—and renowned, those Mitrāvaruṇa

### Printed p. 109

- मही (*mahī*)—she of great nature
- ऋतावरी (*ṛtāvarī*)—she of the nature of truth
- माता (*mātā*)—and mother of the gods
- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—Aditi
- असुराय (*asurāya*)—to destroy the asuras
- जजान (*jajāna*)—bore

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Aditi, of great nature, of the nature of truth and mother of the gods, bore Mitrāvaruṇa, who know the whole world of moving and unmoving things, who are of the most excellent splendour, and who are renowned, in order to destroy the asuras.

##### English translation
The great and veracious Aditi, the mother (of the gods), gave birth to those two who are possessed of all affluence, and shinnig with great splendour, for the (destruction of the) Asuras.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒हांता॑ मि॒त्रावरु॑णा स॒म्राजा॑ दे॒वावसु॑रा ।  
> ऋ॒तवा॑नावृ॒तमा घो॑षतो बृ॒हत् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> महांता । मित्रावरुणा । संराजा । देवा । असुरा ।  
> ऋतवान् । ऋतं । आ । घोषतः । बृहत् ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> महांता गुणाधिक्येन महांतौ सम्राजा सम्राजौ सम्यग्दीप्यमानावसुरावसुरौ बलवंतौ । यद्वा । सर्वांतर्यामितया प्रेरकौ । ऋतानाना सत्यवंतौ
> मित्रावरुणौ देवौ बृहत् स्तोत्रशस्त्रादिना महांतमृतं यज्ञमा घोषतः ।
> स्वदीप्त्या प्रकाशयतः ॥ घुषेर्लट रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

महांता (*mahāṃtā*): great through the abundance of their qualities. सम्राजा (*samrājā*): shining well. असुरा (*asurā*): powerful; or else, impellers, as the inner controllers of all. Mitrāvaruṇa, the truthful gods, make the great ṛta, the sacrifice, resound and shine by their own brightness, through stotra, śastra and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महांता (*mahāṃtā*)—those of exceedingly great qualities
- सम्राजा (*samrājā*)—those who shine exceedingly
- असुरा (*asurā*)—the powerful
- देवौ (*devau*)—of divine nature
- मित्रावरुणा (*mitrāvaruṇā*)—Mitrāvaruṇa

### Printed p. 110

- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—having acquired a great nature through stotra, śastra and the like
- यतं (*yataṃ*)—the sacrifice
- आ घोषतः (*ā ghoṣataḥ*)—they make shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Mitrāvaruṇa, who are of exceedingly great qualities, who shine exceedingly, who are powerful and of divine nature, make shine the sacrifice, which has acquired a great nature through stotra, śastra and the like.

##### English translation
The great Mitra and Varuna, the two sovereign and powerful deities, the observers of truth, illumne our solemn rite.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नपा॑ता॒ शव॑सो म॒हः सू॒नू दक्ष॑स्य सु॒क्रतू॑ ।  
> सृ॒प्रदा॑नू इ॒षो वास्त्वधि॑ क्षितः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नपाता । शवसः । महः । सूनू इति । दक्षस्य । सुक्रतू इति सुक्रतू ।  
> सृप्रदानू इति सृप्रदानू । इषः । वास्तु । अधि । क्षितः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> महो महतः शवसो बलस्य नपाता नपात् पौत्रौ । बलत उत्पादिताविति
> बलस्य पौत्रौ । तादृशौ दक्षस्य । दक्ष वृद्धौ शीघ्रार्थे चेति दक्षो वेगः । तस्य सूनू
> पुत्रौ । बलाद्वेग इति तयोः पुत्रत्वं । ता सुक्रतू शोभनकर्माणौ सृप्रदानू
> प्रसृतधनादिदानौ मित्रावरुणाविषोऽन्नस्य वास्तु निवासस्थानेऽधि क्षितः । अधिवसतः ॥
> अधिशीजिति वास्तुनः कर्मसंज्ञा । क्षयतेर्लट छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Mitrāvaruṇa are the grandsons (नपाता (*napātā*)) of great strength: since they are produced from strength, they are the grandsons of strength. Such as they are, they are the sons of दक्ष (*dakṣa*), which here means speed (the root means growth and also speed). Speed arises from strength, hence their sonship to it. They are of good action, and liberal in giving wealth and the like widely. Mitrāvaruṇa dwell in the dwelling-place of food (इषः (*iṣaḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)—to strength
- नपाता (*napātā*)—grandsons
- दक्षस्य (*dakṣasya*)—to speed
- सूनू (*sūnū*)—sons
- सुक्रतू (*sukratū*)—those of excellent action
- सुप्रदानू (*supradānū*)—those who give widely, Mitrāvaruṇa
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—to food
- वास्तु (*vāstu*)—in the dwelling-place
- अधि क्षितः (*adhi kṣitaḥ*)—they dwell (they are the lords)

### Printed p. 111

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Mitrāvaruṇa, grandsons to great strength, sons to speed, of excellent action and liberal in giving, are lords of food and dwell in its dwelling-place.

##### English translation
Grandsons of mighty strength, sons of energy, doers of good deeds liberal benefactors, they preside over the habitation of food.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सं या दानू॑नि ये॒मथु॑र्दि॒व्याः पार्थि॑वी॒रिषः॑ ।  
> नभ॑स्वती॒रा वां॑ चरंतु वृ॒ष्टयः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सं । या । दानूनि । येमथुः । दिव्याः । पार्थिवीः । इषः ।  
> नभस्वतीः । आ । वां । चरंतु । वृष्टयः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मित्रावरुणौ यौ युवां दानूनि देयानि धनानि सं येमथुः अस्मासु
> संयच्छतं तथा दिव्या दिव्यानि दिवि भवानि पार्थिवीः पृथिव्यामुत्पन्नानीषोऽन्नानि
> संयच्छतं । वृष्ट्यभावे कथमन्नं लभ्यत इति चेत् तदुच्यते । नभस्वतीरुदकवत्यो
> वृष्टयस्तादृशौ वां युवामा चरंतु । उपतिष्ठंतु । यदा वृष्ट्यवेला तदा
> वर्षतमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Mitrāvaruṇa, bestow upon us the gifts, the wealth that is to be given; likewise bestow heavenly foods, those that are in the sky, and earthly foods, those born on the earth. If it is asked how food is to be had when there is no rain, the answer is this: may the rains, which are full of water (नभस्वतीः (*nabhasvatīḥ*)), attend upon you both, that is, come to you; when it is time for rain, you rain. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Mitrāvaruṇa) या दानूनि (*yā dānūni*)—the wealth that is to be given to us
- सं येमथुः (*saṃ yemathuḥ*)—provide to us
- दिव्याः (*divyāḥ*)—those produced in the heavenly world
- पार्थिवीः (*pārthivīḥ*)—and those produced on earth
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—provide to us the foods also
- नभस्वतीः (*nabhasvatīḥ*)—full of water
- वृष्टयः (*vṛṣṭayaḥ*)—the rains
- वां (*vāṃ*)—you
- आ चरंतु (*ā caraṃtu*)—may they serve

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Mitrāvaruṇa, provide to us the wealth that is to be given to us. Provide to us also foods, both those produced in the heavenly world and those produced on earth. May the rains, full of water, serve you.

### Printed p. 112

##### English translation
Bestow (upon us) good gifts, viands, whether of heaven or earth : may the water-shedding rains atten upon you.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अधि॒ या बृ॑ह॒तो दि॒वो॒३॒॑ ३ भि यू॒थेव॒ पश्य॑तः ।  
> ऋ॒तावा॑ना स॒म्राजा॒ नम॑से हि॒ता ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अधि । या । बृहतः । दिवः । अभि । यूथा इव । पश्यतः ।  
> ऋतवाना । संराजा । नमसे । हिता ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> या यौ मित्रावरुणौ बृहतो दिवो द्योतमानान्देवानधि पश्यतः । तत्र
> दृष्टांतः । यूथेव यथा वृषभो गोयूथानि रंतुमभ्यभिमुखं पश्यति
> तद्वदेत् स्ववीर्येणासुरान्हत्वा देवान्मोदयितुं पश्यत इत्यर्थः । कीदृशौ ।
> ऋतावाना सत्यवंतौ सम्राजा सम्राजौ सम्यग्दीप्यमानौ नमसे हविषे हिता हितौ
> प्रियौ पश्यत इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The two, Mitrāvaruṇa, who look upon the great shining gods. The example: as a bull looks towards herds of cows in front of him in order to take his pleasure, so they look, having slain the asuras by their own strength, in order to delight the gods; this is the meaning. Of what kind are they? Truthful, shining well, and dear to the oblation (नमसे (*namase*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतानाना (*ṛtānānā*)—the truthful
- सम्राजा (*samrājā*)—those who shine greatly
- नमसे (*namase*)—to the oblation
- हिता (*hitā*)—dear ones
- या (*yā*)—those Mitrāvaruṇa who are such
- यूथेव अधि (*yūtheva adhi*)—as a bull looks at a herd of cows

### Printed p. 113

- बृहतः (*bṛhataḥ*)—those of great nature
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—shining gods
- अधि पश्यतः (*adhi paśyataḥ*)—they are looking upon

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Those Mitrāvaruṇa who are truthful, shine greatly and are dear to the oblation look upon the gods, who are of great nature and shining, as a bull looks at a herd of cows.

##### English translation
(These are they) who look upon the great deities as (a bull contemplates) the herd, sovereigns observant of truth and propitious to adoration.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒तावा॑ना॒ नि षे॑दतुः॒ साम्रा॑ज्याय सु॒क्रतू॑ ।  
> धृ॒तव्र॑ता क्ष॒त्रिया॑ क्ष॒त्रमा॑शतुः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत्‌वाना । नि । सेदतुः । सांराज्याय । सुक्रतू इति सुक्रतू ।  
> धृतव्रता । क्षत्रिया । क्षत्रं । आशतुः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतावानर्तावान् सत्यवंतौ सुक्रतू शोभनकर्माण् सुप्रज्ञा वा
> मित्रावरुण् साम्राज्याय साम्राज्यार्थं नि षेदतुः । न्यसीदतां । तथा मंत्रः ।
> नि षसाद धृतव्रतो वरुणः पस्त्यास्वा साम्राज्याय सुक्रतुः । ऋ. १-२५-१० । इति ।
> धृतव्रता धृतव्रत् धृतकर्माण् क्षत्रिया क्षत्रियौ बलवंतौ क्षत्रं बलमाशतुः ।
> आनशाते । व्याप्नुत इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya
