> युक्ष । हि । त्वं । रथसहा । युवस्व । पोष्या । वसो इति ।  
> आत् । नः । वायो इति । मधु । पिब । आस्माकं । सवना । आ । गहि ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एतदाद्या वायव्यः । हे वायो रथसहा रथसहौ रथवहनसमर्थावश्वा ।
> हिरवधारणे । त्वमेव युक्ष । संयोजय । हे वसो वासयितः शत्रोणां
> पोष्या पोष्य कंठेषु करशलास्थालनैराश्वास्य पोषणीयौ तावश्वा युवस्व ।
> संग्रामेषु शत्रुवधार्थं मिश्रय । यद्वा अस्मद्यज्ञेषु संमिश्रय । ताभ्यां
> युक्तः सन् यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छेत्यर्थः । हे वायो आदनंतरं नोऽस्मदीयं मधु
> मदकरं सोमं त्वं पिब । अत एनास्माकं यज्ञेषु सवना त्रिषु सवनेष्वा गहि ।
> सोमपानार्थवागच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Ṛk and the following ones are addressed to Vāyu. O Vāyu, रथसहा (*rathasahā*) are two horses able to draw a chariot; the particle हि (*hi*) is for emphasis. You yourself yoke them (युक्ष (*yukṣa*), join them together). O वसो (*vaso*), who cause (enemies) to dwell **NOTE**: पोष्या (*poṣyā*) are the two horses to be fostered, comforted by pats of the palm on their necks **NOTE**; join (युवस्व (*yuvasva*)) those two horses; mingle them in battles for the slaying of enemies; or else mingle them in our sacrifices. The meaning is: yoked with them, come to the sacrifice. O Vāyu, after that drink our intoxicating (मधु (*madhu*)) Soma. Therefore come to our sacrifices, to the three libations, for drinking Soma.

### Printed p. 152

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वायो (*vāyo*)—O Vāyu
- रथसहा (*rathasahā*)—the horses fit to draw your chariot
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- युक्षु (*yukṣu*)—yoke to the chariot
- वसो (*vaso*)—O Vāyu, who overcome enemies
- पोष्या (*poṣyā*)—the horses that deserve your fostering
- युवस्व (*yuvasva*)—bring together in our houses of sacrifice
- आत् (*āt*)—after that
- नः (*naḥ*)—offered by us
- मधु (*madhu*)—the sweet-making Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- अस्माकं (*asmākaṃ*)—in our sacrifices
- सवना (*savanā*)—in all three times of sacrifice
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be gracious, come for drinking Soma

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Vāyu, yoke to your chariot the horses fit to draw it. O Vāyu, who overcome enemies, bring together in our houses of sacrifice the horses that deserve your fostering. After that drink this sweet-making Soma offered by us. Be gracious, come to our sacrifices, at all three times of sacrifice, for drinking Soma.

##### English translation
Yoke your chariot-drawing horses, Vayu; bring them, Vasu, encouraged (to the sacrifice); then drink our Soma; come to our daily libations.

#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तव॑ वायवृतस्पते॒ त्वष्टु॑र्जामातरद्भुत ।  
> अवां॒स्या वृ॑णीमहे ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तव । वायो इति । ऋतःपते । त्वष्टुः । जामातः । अद्भुत ।  
> ।  
> अवांसि । आ । वृणीमहे ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वायव्ये पशौ वपायास्तव वायवित्येषा वानुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । प्र
> वायुमच्छा बृहती मनीषा तव वायुवृतस्पते । आ. ३-८ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Use: in the animal sacrifice to Vāyu, at the offering of the omentum (vapā), the verse beginning तव वायवित्येषा (*tava vāyavityeṣā*) is the anuvākyā. It is laid down in the sūtra (Ā. 3-8), which cites the verse beginning "pra vāyum" together with this one.

### Printed p. 153

> हे ऋतस्पत ऋतपते यज्ञानां पते ॥ सर्वप्रातिपदिकेभ्यो लालसायां
> सुक् । का. ७-१-५१-४ । इति सुगागमः ॥ त्वष्टुर्जामाशर्ब्रह्मणो जामातः । एषा
> कथेतिहासादिभिरवगंतव्या । तादृशाद्भुत महन् विचित्रकर्मन् हे वायो तव
> त्वदीयान्यवांसि पालनान्या वृणीमहे । वयमस्मिन्शुयागे संभजामहे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O ऋतस्पत (*ṛtaspata*), lord of sacrifices; son-in-law of Tvaṣṭṛ, that is of Brahmā: this is to be understood from tales, legends and the like. Such being the case, O wonderful (अद्भुत (*adbhuta*)), great one of marvellous deeds, Vāyu, we choose (वृणीमहे (*vṛṇīmahe*)) your protections (अवांसि (*avāṃsi*), acts of protecting); we partake of them in this sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यतस्पते (*yataspate*)—protector of sacrifice
- त्वष्टुः (*tvaṣṭuḥ*)—to Tvaṣṭṛ
- जामातः (*jāmātaḥ*)—son-in-law
- अद्भुत (*adbhuta*)—of wonderful prowess
- वायो (*vāyo*)—O Vāyu
- तव (*tava*)—your
- अवांसि (*avāṃsi*)—protections
- आ वृणीमहे (*ā vṛṇīmahe*)—we ask for

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Vāyu, protector of sacrifice, son-in-law of Tvaṣṭṛ, of wonderful prowess, we ask for your protections.

##### English translation
We solicit your protection, Vayu, lord of sacrifice, wonderful son-in-law of Twashtri.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the offering of the omentum in the animal sacrifice made to the deity Vāyu, this Ṛk beginning तव वयो (*tava vayo*) is to be used as the anuvākyā mantra, as is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra प्र वायुमच्छा बृहती मनीषा तव नायवृतस्पते (*pra vāyumacchā bṛhatī manīṣā tava nāyavṛtaspate*) (Ā. 3-8).

#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वष्टु॒र्जामा॑तरं व॒यमीशा॑नं रा॒य ई॑महे ।  
> सु॒तावं॑तो वा॒युं द्यु॒म्ना जना॑सः ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वष्टुः । जावातरं । वयं । ईशानं । रायः । ईमहे ।  
> सुतवंतः । वायुं । द्युम्ना । जनासः ॥ २२ ॥

### Printed p. 154

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इमे जनासो जना वयं त्वष्टुर्ब्रह्मणो जामातरमीशानं सर्वस्येश्वरं
> एतादृशं वायं सुतवंतोऽभिषुतसोमा रायो धनमीमहे ! याचामहे ।
> तेन दत्तेन वयं द्युम्ना धनवंतः स्यामेति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We, these people (जनासः (*janāsaḥ*)), having Soma pressed (सुतवंतः (*sutavaṃtaḥ*)), ask (ईमहे (*īmahe*), beg) for wealth (रायः (*rāyaḥ*)) from such a Vāyu, the son-in-law of Tvaṣṭṛ, that is of Brahmā, the ruler (ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)) of all. The remainder to be supplied is: by what he gives, may we be possessed of wealth (द्युम्ना (*dyumnā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुतवंतः (*sutavaṃtaḥ*)—those who have Soma pressed and ready for offering
- जनासः वयं (*janāsaḥ vayaṃ*)—we, the sacrificers
- श्वष्टुः (*śvaṣṭuḥ*)—to Brahmā
- जामातरं (*jāmātaraṃ*)—the son-in-law
- ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)—and the lord of all
- वायुं (*vāyuṃ*)—from the god Vāyu
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—wealth
- ई महे (*ī mahe*)—we ask
- द्युम्ना (*dyumnā*)—may we obtain shining wealth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We, the sacrificers who have Soma pressed and ready for offering, ask for wealth from the god Vāyu, the son-in-law of Brahmā and lord of all. May we obtain shining wealth.

##### English translation
We, the offerers of Soma, solicit riches from the sovereign, the son-in-law of Twashtri ; (may we become) wealthy.

#### Ṛk 23

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वायो॑ या॒हि शि॒वा दि॒वो वह॑स्वा॒ सु स्वश्वं ॑।  
> वह॑स्व म॒हः पृ॑थु॒पक्ष॑सा॒ रथे॑ ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वायो इति । याहि । शिव । आ । दिवः । वहस्व । सु । सुऽश्वं ।  
> वहस्व । महः । पृथुपक्षसा । रथे ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वायव्यतृचे वायो याहि शिवा दिव इत्यादिके द्वे ऋचौ द्वितीयातृतीये।
> सूत्रितं च । वायो याहि शिवा दिव इति द्वे । आ. ७-१८ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Vāyavya triplet, the two Ṛks beginning वायो याहि शिवा दिव (*vāyo yāhi śivā diva*), the second and the third, are used; and so it is laid down in the sūtra (Ā. 7-18): the two beginning thus.

### Printed p. 155

> हे वायो दिवो द्युलोकस्य शिव ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति द्वितीयाया लुक् ॥
> शिवं कल्याणमा याहि । आप्रापय । सर्वज्योतिषां त्वदाधारत्वात्तेषामाधारो
> भूत्वा द्युलोके तानि स्थापयेति प्रार्थयते । ततस्त्वं स्वश्वं । अश्वानां
> संघोऽश्वः । शोभनाश्वसंघं रथं सु सुष्टु वहस्व । सर्वतो दिक्षु प्रापय ।
> इदानीं तेभ्योऽपि समर्थावश्वानावह । महो महांस्त्वं पृथुपक्षसा पृथुपार्श्वद्वययुक्तावश् रथे स्वकीये वहस्व । शत्रुहननार्थं संयोजय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa takes the address to Vāyu to mean: O Vāyu, of the heaven-world, bring (āyāhi) what is auspicious (śiva), that is, what is good; establish it. Since all the luminaries rest on you, he prays that Vāyu become their support and set them in heaven. Then Vāyu is asked to carry well (su vahasva) the chariot that has a fine team of horses (the word for horse being a collection of horses), conveying it in all directions. Now bring horses that are capable even for those luminaries. Mahaḥ means "you who are great"; the two horses are broad-flanked, that is, furnished with two broad sides. Yoke them to your own chariot, for the slaying of enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वायोः (*vāyoḥ*)—O Vāyu
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—in the heaven-world
- शिव (*śiva*)—happiness, auspiciousness
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—establish
- स्वश्वं (*svaśvaṃ*)—your excellent horse
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly
- वहस्व (*vahasva*)—cause (it) to travel in all the directions
- मह (*maha*)—you who are great
- पृथु (*pṛthu*)—your horses with broad flanks
- रथे (*rathe*)—to your chariot
- वहस्व (*vahasva*)—yoke

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Vāyu, establish happiness in heaven. Cause your excellent horse to travel exceedingly, in all the directions. You who are great, yoke your horses with broad flanks to your chariot.

##### English translation
Establish, Vayu, happiness in heaven : bear quickly your well-horsed (chariot) ; do you, who are mighty, yoke the broad-flanked (horses) to the car.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

This Ṛk, वायो याहि शिवा दिवः (*vāyo yāhi śivā divaḥ*), and the Ṛk that follows it are to be recited as the second and the third Ṛk of the Vāyavya triplet (the three Ṛks addressed to Vāyu), as it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, वायो याहि शिवा दिव इति द्वे (*vāyo yāhi śivā diva iti dve*) (Ā. 7-10). **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 24

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वां हि सु॒स्सर॑स्तमं नृ॒षद॑नेषु हू॒महे॑ ।  
> ग्रावा॑णं॒ नाश्व॑पृष्ठं मं॒हना॑ ॥ २४ ॥

### Printed p. 156

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्सा० । हि । सुप्सरःतमं । नृसदनेषु । हूमहे ।  
> ग्रावाणं । न । अश्वपृष्ठं । मंहना ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वायव्ये पशौ पुरोडाशहविषोस्तां हि सुप्सरस्तममिति द्वे अनुवाक्ये !
> सूत्रितं च । त्वां हि सुप्सरस्तममिति द्वे कुविदंग नमसा ये वृधासः । आ. ३-८। इति॥
> हे वायो सुप्सरस्तमं । सुप्स इति रूपनाम ॥ रो मत्त्वर्थीयः ॥
> अतिशयेन शोभनरूपवंतं मुंहना स्वकीयेन महत्वेनाश्वपृष्ठं सर्वतो
> व्याप्तपृष्ठं । पृष्ठशब्दः सर्वांगं लक्षयति । व्याप्तकृत्सांगमित्यर्थः । त्वां ।
> हिरवधारणे । त्वामेव नृषदनेषु नृसदनेषु । नरोऽध्वरस्य नेतार ऋत्विजोऽत्र
> सीदंतीति नृषदना यज्ञाः । तेषु हूमहे । वयमाह्वयामः । कथमिव ।
> ग्रावाणं न । यथा सोमाभिषवार्थं ग्रावाणं स्तुतिभिराह्वयंति तद्वृत्तां
> स्तुतिभिराह्वयामः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the animal sacrifice to Vāyu, at the offering of the puroḍāśa and of the havis, these two Ṛks, beginning त्वां हि सुप्सरस्तमं (*tvāṃ hi supsarastamaṃ*), are the two anuvākyā verses; the sūtra is cited as Ā. 3-8. O Vāyu, you who are सुप्सरस्तमं (*supsarastamaṃ*): सुप्स (*supsa*) is a name for form, and the affix denotes possession, so the word means one who has an exceedingly beautiful form. By your own greatness you are अश्वपृष्ठं (*aśvapṛṣṭhaṃ*), one whose back extends everywhere; the word for back indicates the whole body, so the meaning is "one whose whole body is pervading". The word for "you" is emphasised by the particle: you alone we call in the nṛṣadanas, the places where men sit, namely the leaders of the rite, the priests; these are the sacrifices. We invoke you there. How? Like the grāvan: as the priests call the pressing-stone with praises for the pressing of Soma, so we call you with praises.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुप्सरस्तमं (*supsarastamaṃ*)—you who have an exceedingly charming form (O Vāyu)
- मंहना (*maṃhanā*)—by your own greatness
- अश्वपृष्ठं (*aśvapṛṣṭhaṃ*)—you who have limbs extending everywhere
- त्वा हि (*tvā hi*)—you alone
- नृषदनेषु (*nṛṣadaneṣu*)—in the sacrificial houses
- ग्रावाणं न (*grāvāṇaṃ na*)—as one calls the stones that press the Soma, praying to them
- हूमहे (*hūmahe*)—we call, praising

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Vāyu, you who have an exceedingly charming form, whose limbs by your own greatness extend everywhere, you alone we call in the sacrificial houses with praise, as one calls the stones that press the Soma, praying to them.

##### English translation
We invoke you who are of graceful form, extending through your magnitude your limbs in all directions, to our religious rites, like the stone (for bruising the Soma.)

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

This Ṛk, त्वां हि सुप्सरस्तमं (*tvāṃ hi supsarastamaṃ*), and the Ṛk that follows it are to be recited, in order, as the anuvākyā mantras at the offering of the puroḍāśa and of the havis in the animal sacrifice addressed to Vāyu,

### Printed p. 157
as it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, त्वां हि सुप्सरस्तममिति द्वे कुविदंग नमसा ये वृधासः (*tvāṃ hi supsarastamamiti dve kuvidaṃga namasā ye vṛdhāsaḥ*) (Ā. 3-8).

#### Ṛk 25

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स त्वं नो॑ देव॒ मन॑सा॒ वायो॑ मंदा॒नो अ॑ग्रि॒यः ।  
> कृ॒धि वाजा॑ अ॒पो धियः॑ ॥ २५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । त्वं । नः । देव । मनसा । वायो इति । मंदानः । अग्रियः ।  
> कृधि । वाजान् । अपः । धियः ॥ २५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शुनासीरीये स त्वं नो देवेत्येषा वायोरनुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । स त्वं
> नो देव मनसेशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट् । आ. २-२० । इति ॥
> हे देव द्योतमान यद्वा स्तोतव्य वायो अग्रियो देवानां मध्ये
> मुख्योऽग्रतो गंतासि । तादृशस्त्वं मनसा मंदानो मंदमानः स्वयमेव
> मोदमानः सन्नोऽस्माकं वाजानन्नान्यपो मेघभेदनेनोदकानि च उभयस्मिंस्त्वया प्रदत्ते सति धियोऽग्निहोत्रादिकर्माणि च कृधि । कुरु । कारयेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Śunāsīrīya (iṣṭi), the Ṛk स त्वं नो देव (*sa tvaṃ no deva*) is the anuvākyā verse of Vāyu; the sūtra is cited as Ā. 2-20. O deva, shining one, or else, O Vāyu who are to be praised, you are agriya: chief among the gods, the one who goes at the front. Being such, rejoicing in your mind, delighting yourself, give us vājas, that is, foods, and apas, that is, waters, by the splitting of the cloud; and when both have been given by you, make (kṛdhi: do, or cause to be done) the dhiyas, the rites such as the agnihotra.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देव (*deva*)—O shining one
- वायो (*vāyo*)—O Vāyu
- अग्रियः (*agriyaḥ*)—chief among all the gods
- सः त्वं (*saḥ tvaṃ*)—you who are renowned
- मनसा (*manasā*)—in your own mind
- मंदानः (*maṃdānaḥ*)—experiencing delight
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वाजान् (*vājān*)—foods
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—the waters of rain, and giving them
- धियः (*dhiyaḥ*)—our sacrificial rites
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—make fruitful

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O shining Vāyu, you who are renowned and chief among all the gods, experiencing delight in your own mind, give us foods and the waters of rain, and make our sacrificial rites fruitful.

##### English translation
Divine Vayu, foremost (of the gods), exulting in your own mind, cause our rites to be productive of food and water.

### Printed p. 158

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the iṣṭi called Śunāsīrīya, this Ṛk, स त्वं नो देव (*sa tvaṃ no deva*), is to be recited as the anuvākyā mantra addressed to Vāyu, as it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, स त्वं देव मनसेशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट् (*sa tvaṃ deva manaseśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ*) (Ā. 2-20). **NOTE**

## Sūkta 27 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निरुक्थ इति द्वाविंशत्यृचं सप्तमं सूक्तं । आत्रानुक्रमणिका । अग्निरुक्थॆ
> द्व्यधिका मनुर्नैवस्वतो वैश्वदेवं ह प्रागाधमिति । विवस्वतः पुत्रो मनुर्यषिः ।
> प्रथमातृतीयाद्ययुजो बृहत्यो द्वितीयाचतुर्थ्यादियुजः सतोबृहत्यः ।
> इदमादीनां चतुर्णां सूक्तानां विश्वे देवा देवता ॥ सूक्तविनियोगो
> लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

अग्निरुक्थॆ (*agnirukthè*) is the seventh sūkta, of twenty-two Ṛks. The Anukramaṇikā says: अग्निरुक्थॆ द्व्यधिका मनुर्नैवस्वतो वैश्वदेवं ह प्रागाधमिति (*agnirukthè dvyadhikā manurnaivasvato vaiśvadevaṃ ha prāgādhamiti*). The seer is Manu, the son of Vivasvat. The first, the third and the other odd Ṛks are Bṛhatī, and the second, the fourth and the other even Ṛks are Satobṛhatī. The deity of the four sūktas beginning with this one is the Viśve Devāḥ (All-gods). The use of the sūkta in ritual is by indication (laiṅgika). **NOTE**

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta, अग्निरुक्थॆ (*agnirukthè*), is the seventh sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. There are twenty-two Ṛks in it. The Anukramaṇikā says: अग्निरुक्थॆ द्वैधिका मनुर्वैवस्वतो वैश्वदेवं ह प्रागाथं (*agnirukthè dvaidhikā manurvaivasvato vaiśvadevaṃ ha prāgāthaṃ*). Manu, the son of the ṛṣi Vivasvān, is the seer of this sūkta; the Viśve Devāḥ are the deity of this sūkta and of the three sūktas that follow; the Ṛks of this sūkta of odd number, one, three and so on, are in the metre Bṛhatī; those of even number, two, four and so on, are in the metre Satobṛhatī. The use of the sūkta is by indication.

##### Particulars

Sūkta 27. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 4, Sūkta 27. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Vargas 31, 32, 33, 34. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 22. Seer: Manu Vaivasvata. Deity: the Viśve Devāḥ. Metre: Prāgātha (odd Ṛks Bṛhatī, even Ṛks Satobṛhatī).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निरु॒क्थे पु॒रोहि॑तो॒ ग्रावा॑णो ब॒र्हिर॑ध्व॒रे ।  
> ऋ॒चा या॑मि म॒रुतो॒ ब्रह्म॑ण॒स्पतिं॑ दे॒वा अवो॒ वरे॑ण्यं ॥

### Printed p. 159

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निः । उक्ते । पुरःहितः । ग्रावाणः । बर्हिः । अध्वरे ।  
> यचा । यामि । मरुतः । ब्रह्मणः । पतिं । देवान् । अवः । वरेण्यं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मनुः प्रार्थयते। उक्थ्ये स्तोत्रशस्त्रात्मकेऽध्वरे हिंसारहितेऽस्मिन्यज्ञेऽग्निः
> पुरोहितो यज्ञार्थं पुरुत उत्तरवेद्यामृत्विग्निर्हिहितोऽभूत् । तथा ग्रावाणश्च
> सोमाभिषवार्थं पुरुतो निहिताः । बर्हिश्च पुरतो निहितमासादितं । एवं
> सामग्रां सत्यां मरुत एकोनपंचाशन्मरुद्धणान् ब्रह्मणस्पतिं स्तोत्रस्य पालयितारमेतन्नामकं देवं देवानिंद्रादींश्च एतान् सर्वानेवान्वरेण्यं वरणीयं
> भजनीयमवो रक्षणमृचा सूक्तरूपया स्तुत्या यामि । मनुरहं याचामि ॥
> याचतेर्लट रूपं । वर्णलोपश्कांदसः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Manu prays. In this sacrifice, free from violence and made up of stotra and śastra, Agni has been placed in front as purohita for the sacrifice (the words about the northern altar are damaged in the text). **NOTE** Likewise the pressing-stones have been set in front for the pressing of Soma, and the sacred grass has been set and spread. When the materials are thus ready, I, Manu, ask with the ṛc, that is, with praise in the form of a sūkta, for desirable, adorable protection from the Maruts (the forty-nine hosts of Maruts), from Brahmaṇaspati, the protector of the stotra, a deity of that name, and from the gods, Indra and the others, all of these.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उक्ते (*ukte*)—in the sacrifice that is accompanied by stotra and śastra mantras
- अध्वरे (*adhvare*)—in the sacrifice
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- पुरोहितः (*purohitaḥ*)—is the purohita
- ग्रावाणः (*grāvāṇaḥ*)—the pressing-stones
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—the sacred grass seat, are ready for the rite (while these materials are ready)
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Maruts
- ब्रह्मणस्पतिं (*brahmaṇaspatiṃ*)—Brahmaṇaspati
- देवान् (*devān*)—the gods, Indra and the others
- यचा (*yacā*)—praying with praise in the form of a sūkta
- वरेण्यं (*vareṇyaṃ*)—most excellent
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- यामि (*yāmi*)—I ask

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In the sacrifice accompanied by stotra and śastra mantras, Agni is the purohita. The stones that press the Soma and the sacred grass seat are ready for the rite. While these materials are ready, I pray to the Maruts, to Brahmaṇaspati and to Indra and the other gods with praise in the form of a sūkta, and ask for most excellent protection.

##### English translation
Agni is the Purohita at the sacaifice : the stones, the sacred grass (are prepared) for the ceremony. I invoke with the holy verse the Maruts, Brahmanaspati, and all the gods, for their desirable protection.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ प॒शुं गा॑सि पृथि॒वीं वन॒स्पती॑नु॒षासा॒ नक्त॒मोष॑धीः ।  
> विश्वे॑ च नो वसवो विश्ववेदसो धी॒नां भू॑त प्रावि॒तारः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 160

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । पशुं । गासि । पृथिवीं । वनस्पतीन् । उषसा । नक्तं । ओषधीः ।  
> विश्वे । च । नः । वसवः । विश्ववेदसः । धीनां । भूत । प्रअवितारः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वार्धर्चेऽग्निः संबोध्यते पश्वादिशब्दसद्भावात् । हे अग्ने नोऽस्म
> दीये यज्ञे पशुमग्नीषोमायं पशुं प्रत्या गासि । आगच्छसि ॥ गाज् गतौ ।
> व्यत्ययेन परस्मैपदं ॥ तथा पृथिवीमिदं देवसदनं प्रति किंच वनस्पतीन् मथनसाधनानरणिरूपान्ननस्पतीन्प्रति तथोषासा होतव्यत्वेसोषःकालं तथा नक्तं
> यष्टव्यतया रात्रिं च प्रति किंचौषधीः ॥ उष दाहेऽत्र मादनकर्मा ॥ ओषंति
> माद्यंत्यनेनेत्योषः सोमः। स धीयते निधीयते येप्तित्योषधयो ग्रावाणः।
> तान्प्रत्यागच्छसि । यद्वा । ओषधयः फलपाकांता लताः । ताः प्रत्यायाहि । अथवा
> हे स्तोतः पश्वादी ना गासि । समंतात्सुहि ॥ कै गै शब्द इति धातुः ॥ ततो हे
> वसवो वासयितारो विश्ववेदसः सर्वधनाः सर्वज्ञाना वा हे विश्वे सर्वॆऽपि
> देवाः नोऽस्मदीयानां कर्मणां प्रावितारो भूत । अनेनाग्निना सह यूयं
> प्रकर्षेण रक्षका भवत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the first half of the Ṛk Agni is addressed, because of the presence of words such as पशुं (*paśuṃ*). O Agni, to the animal belonging to Agni and Soma in our sacrifice you come (गासि (*gāsi*) means "you come"). Likewise you come to the earth, that is, this place of the gods; to the vanaspatis, the trees in the form of the fire-drills, which are the means of kindling; to Uṣas, the dawn, the time at which the oblation is to be made; and to the night, the time at which the sacrifice is to be performed; and to the oṣadhīḥ. The root here has the sense of intoxicating; oṣa is the Soma, by which they are exhilarated; those in which it is placed are the oṣadhis, the pressing-stones; you come to them. Or else the oṣadhis are the plants that end with the ripening of fruit; you come to those. Or else the word गासि (*gāsi*) is taken as "you sing", from a root meaning "sound", addressed to the praiser (the words of this alternative are damaged). **NOTE** Then, O Vasus, who cause to dwell, and Viśvavedasaḥ, who have all wealth or all knowledge, O all gods, be the protectors of our rites; together with this Agni, be our excellent protectors.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पशुं (*paśuṃ*)—facing the animal belonging to Agni and Soma
- आ गासि (*ā gāsi*)—be gracious **NOTE**
- पृथिवीं (*pṛthivīṃ*)—to this sacrificial ground
- वनस्पतीन् (*vanaspatīn*)—to the trees in the form of fire-drills
- ओषधिः (*oṣadhiḥ*)—to the Soma, facing it
- उषासा (*uṣāsā*)—in the morning
- नक्तं (*naktaṃ*)—in the night (be gracious)
- वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)—givers of wealth
- विश्ववेदसः (*viśvavedasaḥ*)—all-knowing ones
- विश्वे च (*viśve ca*)—O all gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- धीनां (*dhīnāṃ*)—to the rites
- प्रावितारः (*prāvitāraḥ*)—excellent protectors
- भूत (*bhūta*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, be gracious facing the animal belonging to Agni and Soma, this sacrificial ground, the trees in the form of fire-drills and the Soma, in the morning and in the night. O Viśve Devāḥ, givers of wealth and all-knowing, be excellent protectors of our rites.

##### English translation
You come (Agni), to the dwelling (of the worshipper), to the touch-wood, to the Soma, at dawn and the night: Universal deities, givers of wealth, knowing all things, be the defenders of our pious acts.

### Printed p. 161
#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र सू न॑ एतध्व॒रो॒३॒॑३ग्ना दे॒वेषु॑ पू॒र्व्यः ।  
> आ॒दि॒त्येषु॒ प्र वरु॑णे धृ॒तव्र॑ते म॒रुत्सु॑ वि॒श्वभा॑नुषु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । सु । नः । एतु । अध्वरः । अग्ना । देवेषु । पूर्व्यः ।  
> आदित्येषु । प्र । वरुणे । धृतव्रते । मरुत्सु । विश्वभानुषु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्व्यः पुरातनः । पूर्वान् पुरातनानिंद्रादीन् देवान्प्रति क्रियमाणत्वाद्यज्ञोऽपि पूर्व इत्युच्यते । तादृशो मुख्यो नोऽस्मदीयोऽध्वरो
> यज्ञोऽग्ना ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति सप्तम्या डादेशः ॥ अग्नौ । अग्निर्देवानां मुख्यत्वात्पथममभिहितः । तस्मिन्नग्नौ सर्वॆष्टन्येषु देवेषु च सु सुष्टु प्रैतु ।
> प्रकर्षण गच्छतु । देवान् विशिनष्टि । आदित्येष्टदितेः पुत्रेष्ट्विंद्रादिषु धृतव्रते
> धृतकर्मणि वरुणे च विश्वभानुषु सर्वतो व्याप्ततेजस्केषु मरुत्तु च प्रैतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Pūrvya means ancient. Since the sacrifice is performed with reference to the ancient gods, Indra and the others, the sacrifice too is called ancient. Such a principal sacrifice of ours (adhvara is the sacrifice) is addressed to Agni (the locative अग्ना (*agnā*) stands for अग्नौ (*agnau*)). Agni is named first because he is the chief of the gods. May the sacrifice go well, exceedingly well, to him and to all the other gods as well. Sāyaṇa then specifies the gods: among the Ādityas, the sons of Aditi, Indra and the others; to Varuṇa, who is dhṛtavrata, one who upholds rites; and among the Viśvabhānus, those whose radiance is spread on all sides, namely the Maruts. May the sacrifice go to them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- पूर्वः (*pūrvaḥ*)—ancient and principal
- अध्वरः (*adhvaraḥ*)—the sacrifice
- अग्ना (*agnā*)—facing Agni
- सु (*su*)—in the best manner
- प्र एतु (*pra etu*)—may it go and reach
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—among the remaining gods also
- आदित्येषु (*ādityeṣu*)—among the Ādityas also
- धृतवते (*dhṛtavate*)—in Varuṇa, the regulator of rites
- वरुणे (*varuṇe*)—in Varuṇa also
- विश्वभानुषु (*viśvabhānuṣu*)—those whose radiance spreads everywhere
- मरुत्तु (*maruttu*)—in the Maruts also
- प्र (*pra*)—may it go and reach

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May our ancient and principal sacrifice go and reach Agni in the best manner. May it go and reach the remaining gods also, that is, the Ādityas, Varuṇa the regulator of rites, and the Maruts whose radiance spreads everywhere.

##### English translation
Let the ancient sacrifice proceed first to Agni, then to the gods,—to the Adityas, to Varuna, observant of obligations, to the all-resplendent Maruts.

### Printed p. 162
#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विश्वे॒ हि ष्मा॒ मन॑वे वि॒श्ववे॑दसो॒ भुव॑न्वृ॒धे रि॒शाद॑सः ।  
> अरि॑ष्टेभिः पा॒युभि॑र्विश्ववेदसो॒ यंता॑ नोवृ॒कं छ॒र्दिः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विश्वे । हि । स्म । मनवे । विश्ववेदसः । भुवन् । वृधे । रिशादसः ।  
> अरिष्टेभिः । पायुभिः । विश्ववेदसः । यंत । नः । अवृकं । छर्दिः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्ववेदसः सर्वशो व्याप्तधनाः । बहुधना इत्यर्थः । तादृशा रिशादसो
> रिशतां हिंसतां शत्रूणामसितार उपक्षपयितारो वा विश्वे हि ष्म सर्वॆ खलु देवा
> मनवे । षष्ठ्यर्थे चतुर्थी । मनोर्वृधे वर्धनाय भुवन् । भवंतु । स्तोत्रे
> मनुष्याय धनं दत्वा तं वधयंतित्याशास्त्रे । ततो हे विश्ववेदसः सर्वधनाः
> सर्वज्ञा वा देवाः अरिष्टेभिः परैरहिंसितैः पायुभिः पालनैः सहावृकं । व्यकः
> स्तेनः । तद्रहितं । बाधारहितमित्यर्थः । तादृशं छर्दिगृहं नोऽस्मभ्यं यंत ।
> प्रयच्छत । शत्रून्हत्वा गृहेष्टस्माभिः कर्माणि कारयतेश्यर्थः ॥ यंतेति यमेर्लोटि छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् । तस्य तबादेशः । तेनानुनासिकलोपाभावः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Viśvavedasaḥ are those whose wealth is spread on all sides, that is, those of much wealth. Such are the rishādasaḥ, the devourers or those who destroy the enemies who do harm. All the gods, for Manu (the dative has the sense of the genitive), be for the increase of Manu. Having given wealth to a man who praises them, they make him prosper, so the text says. **NOTE** Then, O gods, all-wealthy or all-knowing, together with unharmed protections, that is, guardians not injured by others, give us a chardis, a house, that is avṛka. A vṛka is a thief, so the word means free from him, free from hindrance. The meaning is: having slain the enemies, cause rites to be performed by us in our house.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्ववेदसः (*viśvavedasaḥ*)—those who possess all wealth
- रिशादसः (*riśādasaḥ*)—destroyers of enemies
- विश्वे हि ष्म (*viśve hi ṣma*)—all the gods
- मनवे (*manave*)—of Manu
- वृधे (*vṛdhe*)—for the increase
- भुवन् (*bhuvan*)—may they be close beside him **NOTE**
- विश्ववेदसः (*viśvavedasaḥ*)—O gods who know all things
- अरिष्टेभिः (*ariṣṭebhiḥ*)—not harmed by others
- पायुभिः (*pāyubhiḥ*)—with protections
- अवृकं (*avṛkaṃ*)—free from harm
- छर्दिः (*chardiḥ*)—a house
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- यंत (*yaṃta*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May all the gods, who possess all wealth and destroy enemies, be close beside Manu for his increase. O gods who know all things, give us a house free from harm, together with protections that no one has harmed.

##### English translation
May the universal deities possessors of all opulence, destroyers of foes, be (nigh) to Manu for his prosperity : do you, who know all things,

### Printed p. 163
secure to us an abode safe from robbers through your unassailable protections.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ अ॒द्य सम॑नसो॒ गंता॒ विश्वे॑ स॒जोष॑सः ।  
> ऋ॒चा गि॒रा मरु॑तो॒ देव्यदि॑ते॒ सद॑ने॒ पस्यॆ॑ महि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । अद्य । समनसः । गंत । विश्वे । सजोषसः ।  
> ऋचा । गिरा । मरुतः । देवि । आदिते । सदने । पस्यॆ । महि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> समनसः सर्वेषु स्तोत्रेषु समानमनस्का विश्वे सर्वॆ देवा यूयं
> सजोषसः परस्परं संगताः संतो गिरा । शुशूषुतया 'प्राप्तव्ययेत्यर्थः ।
> तयर्चा सहाद्यास्मिन्यागदिने नो यष्टनस्माना गंते । आगच्छत । अनंतरं हे
> मरुतो देवि द्योतमाने महि महति देवानां मातृत्वान्महत्त्वयुक्ते हे
> आदितेऽदीन एतन्नामिके देवि सदने स्थाने पस्यॆऽस्मदीये गृहे स्तोतव्यतयोपविशत ॥ मरुत इत्यादर्वाक्यभेदादनिघातः ! उत्तरत्र पूर्वस्यामंत्रितस्याविद्यमानवत्त्वेन वाक्यादित्वादनिघातः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O all gods, of one mind in all the stotras and united with one another, come to us today with the praise, that is, with the ṛc, to this sacrifice (the words explaining the praise are damaged). **NOTE** Then, O Maruts; O shining one, the great one, who are endowed with greatness because she is the mother of the gods; O Aditi, goddess so named: sit in the place, in our house, as those who are to be praised.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- समनसः (*samanasaḥ*)—of one mind with regard to the praiser
- विश्वे (*viśve*)—O Viśve Devāḥ
- सजोषसः (*sajoṣasaḥ*)—you who have come together
- गिरा (*girā*)—in the form of praise
- ऋचा (*ṛcā*)—with the Ṛk
- अद्य (*adya*)—at this time of the sacrifice
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ गंत (*ā gaṃta*)—be pleased to come
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- देवि (*devi*)—who are divine in nature
- महि (*mahi*)—who are of great nature
- अदिते (*adite*)—O goddess Aditi
- सदसे (*sadase*)—in the place of the sacrifice
- पस्यॆ (*pasyè*)—be seated in our house

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Viśve Devāḥ, of one mind with regard to the praiser, come together to us at this time of the sacrifice with the Ṛk, which is in the form of praise. O Maruts, and O goddess Aditi, divine in nature and of great nature, be seated in our house, the place of the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 164

##### English translation
Universal deities, united together and of one mind, come this day to us, (attracted) by the sacred praise addressed to you ; and do you, Maruts, and the mighty goddess Aditi, (come) to the dwelling, (our) abode

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि प्रि॒या म॑रुतो॒ या वो॒ अश्वा॑ ह॒व्या मि॑त्र प्रया॒थन॑ ।  
> आ ब॒र्हिरिंद्रो॒ वरु॑णस्तु॒रा नर॑ आदि॒त्यासः॑ सदंतु नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । प्रिया । मरुतः । या । वः । आश्वा । हव्या । मित्र । प्रयार्थन ।  
> आ । बर्हिः । इंद्रः । वरुणः । तुराः । नरः । आदित्यासः । सदंतु । नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मरुतः प्रिया प्रियाणि या यानि वो युष्माकमश्वाश्च्यानि प्रियानश्वसंघानभि प्रयाथन । अस्मद्यज्ञं प्रति प्रापयत । यूयमश्वैर्युक्ताः संत
> आगच्छतेत्यर्थः । आथ हे मित्र । मित्रशब्देनान्ये वरुणादयोऽप्युच्यंते । हे
> मित्रादयो देवाः हव्या हव्यानि हवनयोग्यानि हवींषि स्वीकर्तुमागच्छतेत्यर्थः।
> सूयमाना आगच्छत ॥ प्रपूर्वाद्यातेर्लोटि तप्तनप्तनथनाश्चति धनादेशः ॥ अगत्य
> चेंद्रो वरुण इंद्रावरुणौ तुराः संग्रामे शत्रुवधार्थं त्वरमाणा नरो नेतार
> आदित्यासोऽदितेः पुत्रा मरुदादयो देवाश्च नोऽस्मदीये यज्ञॆ बर्हिबर्हिप्यासादित आ सदंतु । आसीदंतु । प्रविशंतु ॥ सदेः सीदादेशाभावश्चांदसः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, the horses of yours that are dear, those teams of horses, bring them to our sacrifice; come yoked to your horses. Then, O Mitra: by the word Mitra the others, Varuṇa and the rest, are also addressed. O gods, Mitra and the others, come to accept the oblations that are fit for the offering (the words that follow, beginning सूयमाना (*sūyamānā*), are unclear). **NOTE** Further, Indra and Varuṇa (the Indra-Varuṇa pair), who hasten in battle to slay the enemies, the leaders, the Ādityas, the sons of Aditi, and the Maruts and the other gods, sit on the sacred grass that has been laid in our sacrifice; enter it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- या (*yā*)—which horses
- वः (*vaḥ*)—to you
- प्रिया (*priyā*)—are dear, such
- अश्वा (*aśvā*)—horses
- अभि प्रयाथन (*abhi prayāthana*)—urge on to our sacrifice
- मित्र (*mitra*)—O Mitra
- हव्या (*havyā*)—be pleased to accept our oblations
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- तुराः (*turāḥ*)—those who are skilled in battle **NOTE**
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—leaders
- आदित्यासः (*ādityāsaḥ*)—the Ādityas
- नः (*naḥ*)—in this sacrifice of ours
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—on the sacred grass
- आ सदंतु (*ā sadaṃtu*)—let them sit

### Printed p. 165

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, urge the horses that are dear to you to come to our sacrifice. O Mitra, be pleased to accept our oblations. May Indra and Varuṇa, and the Ādityas, who are skilled in battle and are leaders, sit on the sacred grass in this sacrifice of ours.

##### English translation
Direct, Maruts, your beloved horses (to our rite) : Mitra, (come to our) oblations; and may Indra, and Varuna and the swift leaders, the the Adityas, sit down on our sacred grass.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> व॒यं वो॑ वृ॒क्तब॑र्हिषो हि॒तप्र॑यस आनु॒षक् ।  
> सु॒तसो॑मासो वरुण हवामहे मनु॒ष्पदि॒द्दाग्न॑यः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वयं । वः । वृक्तबर्हिषः । हितप्रयसः । अनुषक् ।  
> सुतसोमासः । वरुण । हवामहे । मनुष्यत् । इद्दआग्नयः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वरुण वरुणादयो हे देवाः व्यक्तबर्हिष ऋत्विजो वयमानुषक्
> सुगादिष्पनुषक्तं यथा भवति तथा हितप्रयसः । प्रीणातीति प्रयोऽन्नं । तेषु
> निहितहविष्काः संतो वो युष्मान् हवामहे । एतानि हवींप्यादातुमाह्वयामः।
> कीदृशाः । सुतसोमासोऽभिषुतसोमा इद्दाग्नय आहुतिभिः समिद्धाग्नयो
> वयमाह्वयामः । तत्र दृष्टांतः । मनुष्वत् । मनुर्यथा यज्ञे युष्मानाजुहाव तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Varuṇa and the other gods, we priests, with the sacred grass trimmed, in due order and with foods set out (prayas is food, that which pleases), having laid oblations in those foods, call you to take these oblations. What kind of priests? Those who have pressed the Soma and those who have kindled the fires with oblations. We invite you; the example is manuṣvat: as Manu called you in the sacrifice, so do we. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरुण (*varuṇa*)—O Varuṇa and the other gods
- व्यक्तबर्हिषः (*vyaktabarhiṣaḥ*)—those who prepare and make ready the sacred grass seat
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—in due order
- हितप्रयसः (*hitaprayasaḥ*)—who have the oblations that are offered
- सुतसोमासः (*sutasomāsaḥ*)—who have pressed and prepared the Soma
- इद्दाग्नयः (*iddāgnayaḥ*)—who have caused the fire to blaze with oblations

### Printed p. 166
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we (the priests)
- वः (*vaḥ*)—you
- मनुष्वत् (*manuṣvat*)—as Manu in former times prayed to you and called you
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we also call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa and the other gods, we priests, who have prepared the sacred grass seat, who have the oblations to offer to you in due order, who have pressed and prepared the Soma and who have caused the fire to blaze with oblations, call you to our sacrifice as Manu in former times praised and called you.

##### English translation
Bearing the clipped sacred grass, offering in due order the(sacrificial) food, presenting the effused Soma, and having the fires kindled, we invoke you, Varuna, (and the rest), as did Manu.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ प्र या॑त॒ मरु॑तो॒ विष्णो॒ अश्वि॑ना॒ पूष॒न्माकी॑नया धि॒या ।  
> इंद्र॒ आ या॑तु प्रथ॒मः स॑नि॒ष्युभि॒र्वृषा॒ यो वृ॑त्र॒हा गृ॒णे ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । प्र । यात । मरुतः । विष्णो इति । अश्विना । पूषन् । माकीनया । धिया ।  
> इंद्रः । आ । यातु । प्रथमः । सनिष्युभिः । वृषा । यः । वृत्रहा । गृणे ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विश्वे देवाः प्र यात । प्रकर्षणास्मदीयकर्माण्यागच्छत । हे मरुतो
> हे विष्णो स्वबलेन सर्वतो व्याप्तैतन्नामक देव हे अश्विनाश्विन् पूषन् ।
> स्तोत्थानादिना पोषयतीति पूषा । एतन्नामक देव मरुदादयो देवाः
> माकीनया ॥ अस्मच्छब्दाद्युष्मदस्मदोः । पा. ४-३-१ । इति खञ् शैषिकः । ऐकदचने
> तवकममकाविति ममकादेशः । वर्णलोपश्रांदसः ॥ मया क्रियमाणया धिया
> स्तुत्या सहास्मद्यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छत ॥ मरुदादेरामंत्रितस्य वाक्यभेदादनिघातः ॥
> किंच प्रथमो देवानां मुख्यः स इंद्रश्चा यातु । वृषा कामानां सेक्ता य
> इंद्रः सनिष्युभिः । सनिः संभजनं । तदात्मन इच्छद्भिः स्तोतृभिर्वत्रहापामावरकस्य
> वृत्रासुरस्य हंतेति गृणे स्तूयते ॥ गृ शब्द इत्यस्य कर्मणि लिट छांदसो विकरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Viśve Devāḥ, come forth; come to our rites. O Maruts; O Viṣṇu, the deity so named, who pervades everywhere by his own strength; O Aśvins; O Pūṣan, who nourishes by praise and the like, the deity so named: and the Maruts and the other gods, come to our sacrifice with the praise made by me, माकीनया (*mākīnayā*) (that is, made by me). Further, Indra also, first, that is, chief among the gods, let him come: he is the showerer, the one who pours out the desires. Sanisyubhiḥ means by praisers who desire the sharing (sani is partaking) of that. He is the slayer of Vṛtra, the demon who covers the waters, and so he is praised.

### Printed p. 167

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—O Maruts
- विष्णो (*viṣṇo*)—O Viṣṇu
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvin deities
- पूषन् (*pūṣan*)—O god Pūṣan
- माकीनया (*mākīnayā*)—recited by me
- धिया (*dhiyā*)—pleased with the praise
- आ प्रयात (*ā prayāta*)—all of you, come
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—the showerer of desires
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- सनिष्युभिः (*saniṣyubhiḥ*)—by his worshippers
- वृत्रहा (*vṛtrahā*)—as the slayer of Vṛtra
- गृणे (*gṛṇe*)—is praised, such a one
- प्रथमः (*prathamaḥ*)—chief among the gods
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- आ यातु (*ā yātu*)—let him come to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, O Viṣṇu, O Aśvin deities, O god Pūṣan, pleased with the praise recited by me, come, all of you. May Indra also come to us, Indra who is chief among the gods, the showerer of desires, whom his worshippers praise as the slayer of Vṛtra.

##### English translation
Maruts, Aswins, Pushan, come hither (induced) by my praise : may Indra, the first (of the gods). also come, the showerer (of benefits), he who is praised by (his) worshippers as the slayer of Vritra.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वि नो॑ देवासो अद्रु॒होऽच्छि॑द्रं॒ शर्म॑ यच्छत ।  
> न यद्दू॒राद्व॑सवो॒ नू चि॒दंति॑तो॒ वरू॑थमाद॒धर्ष॑ति ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वि । नः । देवासः । अद्रुहः । अच्छिद्रं । शर्म । यच्छत ।  
> न । यत् । दूरात् । वसवः । नु । चित् । अंतितः । वरूथं । आदधर्षति ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अद्रुहः स्तोतृणामद्रोग्यारः । यद्वा ॥ म्रुहेर्णादिकः कर्मणि
> क्विप् ॥ शत्रुभिरहिंस्याः । हे देवासो मरुदादयो देवाः अच्छिद्रं बाधकरहितं
> साधीयो वा शर्म । शृणाति दुःखादिकमिति शर्म गृहं । तन्नोऽस्मभ्यं वि यॆच्छत ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Adruhaḥ, you who do not injure the praisers, or else you who cannot be harmed by enemies; O gods, Maruts and the others, give us a house (śarma, so called because it breaks sorrow and the like) that is faultless, free from obstruction, or very good. Give that to us.

### Printed p. 168

> हे वसवः शत्रूणां वासयितारो मरुदादयः दूराद्दूरदेशादंतितोऽंतिकदेशाद्वा कश्चिदागत्य नू चित्कदाचिदपि वरूथं वरणीयं संभजनीयं यद्गृहं
> नादधर्षति आधर्षणं हिंसनं न करोति तद्गृहं प्रयच्छतेति समन्वयः ॥ धृष
> प्रसहन इत्यस्य विभाषितणिच त्वात् यदा णिच् नास्ति तदा रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vasus, Maruts and the others, who cause the enemies to dwell: whatever house is such that no one, coming from afar or from near, at any time assails or harms it, that desirable house, to be partaken of, give to us. This is the construction. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्रुहः (*adruhaḥ*)—O you who do no harm
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—O gods, Maruts and the others
- अच्छिद्रं (*acchidraṃ*)—faultless
- शर्म (*śarma*)—a house
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वि यच्छत (*vi yacchata*)—give
- वसवः (*vasavaḥ*)—O Maruts and the others, who overthrow enemies
- यत् (*yat*)—which house
- दूरात् (*dūrāt*)—from afar
- अंतितः (*aṃtitaḥ*)—from a near place also
- नू चित् (*nū cit*)—at any time (by anyone)
- न आदधर्षत् (*na ādadharṣat*)—cannot be harmed, such
- वरूथं (*varūthaṃ*)—give an excellent house

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts and the other gods, who do no harm, give us a house free from fault. O Maruts and the others, who overthrow enemies, give us an excellent house, such that no one, whether from afar or from near, can besiege and harm it.

##### English translation
Unoppressive deities, bestow upon us a mansion without defect, so that, subduers (of foes), no one may injure our defences, whether from afar or nigh.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस्ति॒ हि वः॑ सजा॒त्यं॑ रिशादसो॒ देवा॑सो॒ अस्ताप्यं॑ ।  
> प्र णः॒ पूर्व॑स्मै सुवि॒ताय॑ वोचत म॒क्षू सु॒म्नाय॒ नव्य॑से॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्ति । हि । वः । सजात्यं । रिशादसः । देवासः । अस्ति । अप्यं ।  
> प्र । नः । पूर्वस्मै । सुविताय । वोचत । मक्षु । सुम्नाय । नव्यसे ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 169

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे रिशादसो रिशतां हिंसतामसितारो देवासो देवा द्योतमाना
> मरुदादयः वो युष्माकं सजात्यमस्ति । परस्परं समानजातिभावोऽस्ति खलु ।
> किंचाप्यं । आपिर्बंधुः । तस्य भाव आप्यं । स्तोतृषु स्तुत्यलक्षणेन संबंधाद्वैवस्वतेन मनुना मया स्तोत्रा सह युष्माकं बंधुभावोऽस्ति खलु । ततः
> पूर्वस्मै प्रथमभाविने सुविताय । सुष्टीयते सर्वैरागम्यत इति सुवितोऽभ्युदयः । तस्मै नव्यसे नवीयसे नवतराय सुम्माय च उभयं मक्षु शीघ्रं
> नोऽस्माकं प्र वोचत । प्रकर्षण ब्रूत । अभ्युदयधनानि प्रयच्छतेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O destroyers of the enemies who do harm, O shining gods, Maruts and the others: there is among you the condition of one kind (sajātya); you are of the same class with one another. Further, there is āpya: āpi is a kinsman, and his state is āpya. Through the relation that consists in praise, there is kinship between you and me, the praiser, Manu Vaivasvata. Therefore, for the earlier, first-arising suvita (what all attain well, prosperity), and for the newer, the very new, sumna (happiness): both of these, speak forth to us quickly. The meaning is: give us prosperity and wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रिशादसः (*riśādasaḥ*)—you who harm the enemies
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—O gods
- वः (*vaḥ*)—among you
- सजात्यं (*sajātyaṃ*)—a relation of belonging to one group
- अस्ति हि (*asti hi*)—there is, is there not
- आप्यं (*āpyaṃ*)—your kinship with your praisers
- अस्ति (*asti*)—there is
- पूर्वस्मै (*pūrvasmai*)—that has come from before
- सुविताय (*suvitāya*)—for prosperity
- नव्यसे (*navyase*)—new
- सुम्नाय (*sumnāya*)—for happiness
- मक्षु (*makṣu*)—quickly
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- प्र वोचत (*pra vocata*)—recite the blessing (svasti-vācana)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, who harm the enemies, there is indeed a relation among you of belonging to one group. Your relation with your praisers is also well known. Recite the blessing for us quickly, for the prosperity that has come from before and for new happiness.

##### English translation
There is identity of race among you, deities, destroyers of foes ; there is kindred (with me your worshipper) ; therefore utter at once the command for our former prosperity and for new happiness.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Rishādasaḥ: रिशतां हिंसतामसितारः (*riśatāṃ hiṃsatāmasitāraḥ*). Those who put down, who harm, the enemy people who do harm; that is, those who harm and kill enemies, destroyers of enemies, is the meaning. Yāska. For this word:
रिशादसः । अस्ति हि वः सजात्यं रिशादसो देवासो अस्त्याप्यं । अस्ति हि वः समानजातिता रेशयदारिणो देवाः । अस्त्याप्यं । आप्यमाप्नोषेः ॥ (*riśādasaḥ | asti hi vaḥ sajātyaṃ riśādaso devāso astyāpyaṃ | asti hi vaḥ samānajātitā reśayadāriṇo devāḥ | astyāpyaṃ | āpyamāpnoṣeḥ ‖*) (3. 6-14). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 170
The gods harm and kill the enemy people who needlessly do violence to others. Since this quality is seen in all the gods, it is said that there is a kind of kinship among the gods. The word āpya is derived from the root āpḷ vyāptau, meaning to obtain, and here it indicates the sense of kinship; so the explanation (nirvacana) has been given.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒दा हि व॒ उप॑स्तुतिमि॒दा वा॒मस्य॑ भ॒क्तये॑ ।  
> उप॑ वो विश्ववेदसो नम॒स्कुरा असृ॒क्षन्या॑मिव ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इदा । हि । वः । उपस्तुतिं । इदा । वामस्य । भक्तये ।  
> उप । वः । विश्ववेदसः । नमस्युः । आ । असृक्षि । अन्यां इव ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विश्ववेदसः सर्वधना हे देवाः नमस्युरन्नमिच्छन् मनुरहं वो
> युष्मद्विषयामुपस्तुतिमन्यामिवादृष्टपूर्वामिव स्थितां । कैश्चिदप्यकृतामित्यर्थः ।
> तादृशीमुपस्तुतिमिदा हि । हिरवधारणे । इदानीमेवोपासृक्षि । उपासृजामि ।
> करोमीत्यर्थः । किमर्थ० । वो युष्मत्संबंधिनो वामस्य वननीयस्येदेदानीमेव भक्तये संभजनाय । लाभायेत्यर्थः ॥ अस्पृक्षीति सृजेर्लुजि रूपं ।
> पादादित्वादनिघातः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O all-wealthy gods: I, Manu, namasyu, desiring food, now indeed compose the praise relating to you, a praise that is as if of another, as if unseen before; the meaning is that no one has made it. Why? For the vāma, the desirable thing belonging to you, to partake of it now, that is, for gain.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्ववेदसः (*viśvavedasaḥ*)—O gods who possess all wealth
- नमस्युः (*namasyuḥ*)—I who desire food
- वः (*vaḥ*)—that belongs to you
- वामस्य (*vāmasya*)—and is attractive, wealth and the like
- इदा (*idā*)—now
- भक्तये (*bhaktaye*)—in order to obtain
- वः (*vaḥ*)—that praises you
- उपस्तुतिं (*upastutiṃ*)—the hymn
- अन्यां इव (*anyāṃ iva*)—as if newly made, not recited by others
- इदा हि (*idā hi*)—now
- उप आ अस्पृक्षि (*upa ā aspṛkṣi*)—having made it, I recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods who possess all wealth, I, who desire food, make now the hymn that praises you, as if newly made, not made or recited by others, and recite it now, in order to obtain at once the wealth and the like that belong to you and are attractive.

### Printed p. 171

##### English translation
Deities, possessed of all wealth, I, desirous of offering worship, address to you verily unprecedented praise, for the sake of obtaining desired affluence.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॒ ष्य वः॑ सवि॒ता सु॑प्रणीत॒योऽस्था॑दू॒र्ध्वो वरे॑ण्यः ।  
> नि द्वि॒पाद॒श्चतु॑ष्पादो अ॒र्थिनोऽवि॑श्रन्पतयि॒ष्णवः॑ ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । स्यः । वः । सविता । सुप्रनीतयः । अस्थात् । ऊर्ध्वः । वरेण्यः ।  
> नि । द्विपादः । चतुःऽपादः । अर्थिनः । अविश्रन् । पतॆयिष्णवः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सुप्रणीतयः । शोभनप्रणीति स्तुतिः । शोभनप्रणयनाः शोभनस्तुतयो
> मरुतः वो युष्माकं मध्य ऊर्ध्व ऊर्ध्व० गंता वरेण्यः सर्वैर्वर्णीयः
> संभजनीयः स सविता सर्वस्य स्वकर्मणि प्रेरक एतन्नामकः स देवो यदोदस्थात
> स्वतेजसोद्धतोऽभूत् तदार्थिनो द्विपादो पादद्वययुक्ताः पुरुषाश्चतुष्पादः
> पादचतुष्टययुक्ता आश्वादयः पतयिष्णवः पतनशीलाः दक्षिणश्च न्यविश्रन् ।
> स्वस्वकार्येषु निविशंते । सूर्य उदिते केचन पुरुषा अग्निहोत्रादिकं कुर्वंति
> केचन देवताविषयं स्तोत्रं कुर्वंति । पश्वादयस्तृणादिभक्षणार्थं सर्वत्र
> संचरंति ॥ न्यविश्रन् । निपूर्वाद्विशतेर्लज व्यत्ययेन परस्मैपदं । बहुलं छंदसीति
> रुडागमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Suprāṇītayaḥ, those of good praise, the Maruts: the word means those who have good guidance, good praises. When that Savitṛ, the deity so named, the impeller of everyone in his own work, who goes upward among you, to be chosen and partaken of by all, rises up (udasthāt: has risen, uplifted by his own radiance), then those who are in need, the bipeds, men with two feet, the quadrupeds, horses and the like with four feet, and the flying ones, those that tend to fly, and the birds, enter upon their own tasks. **NOTE** When the sun has risen some men perform the agnihotra and the like, some make praise of the deities, and animals and the rest roam everywhere to eat grass and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुप्रणीतयः (*supraṇītayaḥ*)—O Maruts, worthy of excellent praise
- यदा (*yadā*)—when
- वः (*vaḥ*)—among you
- ऊर्ध्वः (*ūrdhvaḥ*)—who moves upward
- वरेण्यः (*vareṇyaḥ*)—worthy of worship by all
- स्यः सविता (*syaḥ savitā*)—Savitṛ, the impeller of the world
- उदस्थात् (*udasthāt*)—appears with his radiances (then)
- अर्थिनः (*arthinaḥ*)—who strive to accomplish each his own desired ends
- द्विपादः (*dvipādaḥ*)—men who have two feet
- चतुष्पादः (*catuṣpādaḥ*)—horses and the like that have four feet
- हतयिष्णवः (*hatayiṣṇavaḥ*)—the birds that fly about
- नि अविश्रन् (*ni aviśran*)—are intent on their own tasks

### Printed p. 172

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, worthy of excellent praise, when Savitṛ, who moves upward among you, worthy of worship by all, the impeller of the world, the renowned, appears with his radiances, then men who have two feet, horses and the like that have four feet, and the birds that fly about, all striving to accomplish their own desired ends, are intent on their own tasks.

##### English translation
Devoutly praised (Maruts), when the adorable Savitri has risen above you, then bipeds and quadrupeds, and the flying birds seeking (their objects), enter (upon their fuuctions).

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दे॒वंदे॑वं॒ वोऽव॑से दे॒वंदे॑वम॒भिष्ट॑ये ।  
> दे॒वंदे॑वं हुवेम॒ वाज॑सातये गृ॒णंतो॑ दे॒व्या धि॒या ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> देवंऽदेवं । वः । अवसे । देवंऽदेवं । अभिष्टये ।  
> देवंऽदेवं । हुवेम । वाजसातये । गृणंतः । देव्या । धिया ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पंचमेऽहनि प्रउगशस्त्रे देवंदेवमिति वैश्वदेवस्त्रृचः । सूत्रितं च ।
> देवंदेवं बृहदु गायिषे वचः । आ. ७-१२ । इति ॥
> वयं देव्या द्योतमानया धिया स्तुत्या गृणंतः स्तुवंतः संतो वो
> युष्माकं मध्ये देवं देवं दीप्यमानं देवमवसे कर्मरक्षणायाह्वयाम ।
> अनुक्रमेणाह । अभिष्टयेऽभिलषितप्राप्त्यर्थं च देवं देवं वयमाह्सयाम ।
> ततो वाजसातयेऽन्नलाभाय हुवेम । ह्वयाम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

On the fifth day, in the प्रउगशस्त्रे (*praugaśastre*), the triplet beginning देवंदेवमिति (*devaṃdevamiti*) is addressed to the Viśve Devāḥ; the sūtra is cited as Ā. 7-12, beginning देवंदेवं बृहदु गायिषे वचः (*devaṃdevaṃ bṛhadu gāyiṣe vacaḥ*). We, praising with shining praise, call, among you, each shining god for the protection of our rite. He says it in order: for the obtaining of what we desire we call each god; then for the gain of food we call.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- O gods
- देव्या (*devyā*)—with shining
- धिया (*dhiyā*)—praise
- गृणंतः (*gṛṇaṃtaḥ*)—we who praise
- वः (*vaḥ*)—who are among you
- देवंदेवं (*devaṃdevaṃ*)—each god
